> - How, and by when, do you appeal to the Grievance Committee?

<a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre"></a>

### 2.6 Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre

Every work visa applicant gives fingerprints and a photo at a Tasheer centre; Tasheer runs Saudi visa centres <sup>[N114 (2023)](#n114)</sup>. Dhaka: Jamuna Future Park (Ground Floor), KA-244, Kuril, Progoti Shoroni <sup>[N115 (2023)](#n115), [N114 (2023)](#n114)</sup>. Chattogram: World Trade Center Chittagong (2nd Floor), 102/103 Agrabad C/A, Commerce College Road <sup>[N116](#n116), [N114 (2023)](#n114)</sup>.

**You'll need** (Tasheer's list for Pakistani applicants) <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>:

- your passport (valid 6+ months, 2 blank pages) and a copy;
- the Wafid report;
- the printed appointment letter.

1. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-1"></a>**Book free** at [vc.tasheer.com/appointment](https://vc.tasheer.com/appointment): Bangladesh, Appointment, Schedule, then visa type, centre, date and time <sup>[N114 (2023)](#n114), [N28](#n28)</sup>. One slot per person <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>. Pay no website or broker to book <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-2"></a>**Change or cancel** in the Appointment menu up to the day before; a change within 3 days, or a no-show, blocks booking for 7 days <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-3"></a>**Arrive 15 minutes early,** or the slot can be cancelled, moved to the paid Lounge or delayed 7 days <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-4"></a>**At the centre** you get a token, pay the service fee, staff fill in the visa form on the Saudi Foreign Ministry's visa platform, still often called Enjaz, and you give biometrics <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>. Keep the original receipt to collect your passport <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre-item-5"></a>**Track it** on vc.tasheer.com and collect the passport within 30 days, or it goes back to the embassy <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>. The visa is electronic, with a QR code <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>; check it in [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself).

> **Need to know**
> - What is Tasheer's own document list for Bangladeshi applicants?
> - What is Tasheer's own fee for Bangladeshi applicants (what agencies charge: see [If a relative or friend offers you a visa, item 5.4: Let the agency carry the file, in this order](#if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-5))?
> - What are its phone, email and complaint route in Bangladesh?

<a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa"></a>

### 2.7 Step 6: Give the agency your papers for the visa

The agency applies for the visa; agencies ask for <sup>[N117](#n117)</sup>:

- a passport valid 6+ months with two blank pages;
- white-background photos;
- a police clearance certificate;
- the Wafid fit report;
- where your trade needs it, the skill test certificate.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the Saudi Embassy's own document list?
> - Does every trade need police clearance?
> - What is the card service charge for the police clearance fee?

**Get the police clearance certificate yourself** at [pcc.police.gov.bd](https://pcc.police.gov.bd/ords/f?p=500), close to when the agency needs it: a certificate is generally accepted for up to 6 months from issue <sup>[35](#o35), [N118](#n118)</sup>.

*You'll need:*

- a passport valid 3+ months <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>;
- a scan of its information page attested by a first-class gazetted officer <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>;
- if it shows no address, an attested NID, birth registration or ward councillor's certificate <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>;
- a mobile number;
- Tk 1,500 <sup>[35](#o35), [70](#o70)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-item-1"></a>**Register** with your mobile number and click Apply <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>. Forgot the password: SMS PCC PR and your mobile number to 26969 <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-item-2"></a>**Fill in the form** (personal details, then current and permanent addresses) and upload the scans; as your current address give the permanent or emergency address written in your passport, since the police check you at your current address, in that district or city <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>. Check everything on the last page before you submit: nothing can be changed after <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>. Keep the reference number <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-item-3"></a>**Pay Tk 1,500** by treasury challan at Bangladesh Bank or Sonali Bank (code 1-7301-0001-2681), or by card with a service charge <sup>[35](#o35), [70](#o70)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-item-4"></a>**Track it:** SMS PCC S and your reference number to 26969 <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>. It takes about 7-10 working days in Dhaka, 10-15 elsewhere <sup>[N118](#n118)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-item-5"></a>**Collect it** at Ready for Delivery <sup>[N118](#n118)</sup>: from the Metropolitan Police headquarters counter in a metropolitan city, or the Police Clearance Cell at the District Superintendent of Police's office elsewhere <sup>[N118](#n118)</sup>, with a copy of your passport or application; someone collecting for you needs your authorisation letter <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa-item-6"></a>**If delayed,** call 01320001824 or 01320001825 (Sunday to Thursday, 9 am to 5 pm) or email help.pcc@police.gov.bd <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>; DMP in Dhaka: 01320037339, 01320037329 <sup>[71](#o71)</sup>.

*Check it is real:* apply only on pcc.police.gov.bd, never through a middleman; forged certificates have been sold with QR codes leading to a fake site <sup>[N119](#n119)</sup>.

**If the job needs your education certificates,** apostille them on [MyGov](https://www.mygov.bd/services?category=sector&data=service-sector-67): you get an SMS and download the e-Apostille from your MyGov dashboard <sup>[N120](#n120), [72](#o72)</sup>. It costs Tk 200 per page online and takes up to 5 days; track it on [MyGov application status](https://www.mygov.bd/application-latest-status) <sup>[N121](#n121), [72](#o72)</sup>. If it isn't done in 5 working days, contact the Foreign Ministry's e-Apostille office, Segunbagicha, Dhaka (+8802-223381852, dgcnw@mofa.gov.bd) <sup>[72](#o72)</sup>, then complain on [grs.gov.bd](https://www.grs.gov.bd/), the government's complaint system <sup>[N121](#n121)</sup>. Saudi Arabia accepts apostilles without embassy attestation; anyone can check one by its QR code <sup>[N122 (2022)](#n122), [N120](#n120)</sup>.

<a id="pay-no-embassy-fee-or-visa-fee-to-anyone"></a>

#### 2.7.1 Pay no "embassy fee" or "visa fee" to anyone

The embassy charges nothing <sup>[N123 (2022)](#n123)</sup>, the employer pays the visa <sup>[30](#o30)</sup>, and Tasheer takes only its service fee <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>.

<a id="step-7-check-the-visa-yourself"></a>

### 2.8 Step 7: Check the visa yourself

**You'll need:** the visa number (10 digits, different from the application number) or application number, and your passport number <sup>[53](#o53), [N49 (2024)](#n49)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-7-check-the-visa-yourself-item-1"></a>**Online:** on [KSA Visa Track Application](https://ksavisa.sa/track-application), the Saudi Foreign Ministry's platform, enter the number, your passport number and the captcha, and press Track my application <sup>[53](#o53)</sup>. Press View to see your occupation, sponsor, visa type, validity and length of stay <sup>[N49 (2024)](#n49)</sup>. Check again before paying the rest and before flying: the Saudi labour ministry cancels visas applied for on fake demand letters <sup>[N7](#n7)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-7-check-the-visa-yourself-item-2"></a>**At BMET or your DEMO:** take copies of the visa and the passport's visa page to BMET at 89/2 Kakrail, Dhaka-1000, or your DEMO (addresses: see [Step 1, item 1: Register on the Overseas Employment Platform (OEP)](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-1)) <sup>[42](#o42)</sup>; BMET checks it online, free, within 3 working days <sup>[1](#o1), [37](#o37)</sup>. BMET also has a visa-check app on Google Play <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-7-check-the-visa-yourself-item-3"></a>**If the visa is fake, refused or cancelled,** use the complaint route (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)).

> **Need to know**
> - Does a refused work visa come with a reason or appeal?
> - What is the name of BMET's visa-check app?

<a id="step-8-orientation-bmet-clearance-and-the-flight"></a>

### 2.9 Step 8: Orientation, BMET clearance and the flight

<a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo"></a>

#### 2.9.1 Do the compulsory 3-day pre-departure orientation (PDO)

The orientation covers migration, laws, work, safety and health abroad <sup>[37](#o37), [73 (2024)](#o73)</sup>.

**You'll need,**
at TTC Munshiganj <sup>[73 (2024)](#o73)</sup>:

- your OEP registration;
- copies of your passport and visa;
- proof of a bank account in your name (a cheque leaf or deposit slip copy) and one in a family member's.

1. <a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo-item-1"></a>**Enrol** at any TTC or Institute of Marine Technology <sup>[74 (2022)](#o74)</sup>: in the Ami Probashi app verify your passport, open PDO Training, choose a TTC and batch, pay Tk 100 plus a Tk 100 platform charge by bKash or Nagad, and download the admit card, which you show on the first day <sup>[N90](#n90)</sup>; or apply on OEP and download the [enrolment card](https://www.oep.gov.bd/pdo-enrollment-card) <sup>[37](#o37), [75](#o75)</sup>.
2. <a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo-item-2"></a>**Attend all 3 days** (TTC Munshiganj: 9 am to 5 pm) <sup>[73 (2024)](#o73)</sup>.
3. <a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo-item-3"></a>**Download the certificate** from [OEP](https://www.oep.gov.bd/pdo-certificate) <sup>[75](#o75)</sup>; the centre's office corrects errors <sup>[73 (2024)](#o73)</sup>.
4. <a id="do-the-compulsory-3-day-pre-departure-orientation-pdo-item-4"></a>**Get BMET clearance within 3 months** of the certificate's date, or redo the orientation <sup>[74 (2022)](#o74)</sup>.

If anyone offers to get you through a course or clearance for money, call the police on 999, free and open 24 hours <sup>[37](#o37), [66](#o66)</sup>, or complain to BMET (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)) <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>.

<a id="get-bmet-clearance"></a>

#### 2.9.2 Get BMET clearance

BMET clearance is the emigration clearance, on a smart card <sup>[37](#o37), [1](#o1)</sup>.

*Through the agency:* the agency applies with these, and it takes 3 working days <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>:

- the visa copy;
- the contract;
- your attested registration card;
- its undertaking on Tk 300 stamp paper;
- the welfare fee pay order;
- the tax challan.

You pay the Wage Earners' Welfare Board, which collects the Tk 5,500 welfare fee and the one-time Tk 1,000 insurance premium (5 years, Tk 10 lakh on death) <sup>[36](#o36), [N124](#n124), [37](#o37)</sup>. Income tax: Tk 500 for a single visa; group visas Tk 1,200 skilled, Tk 800 unskilled <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>. Download the card from [OEP](https://www.oep.gov.bd/employee-card) <sup>[75](#o75)</sup>.

**On your own,**
with a visa you got yourself (for Saudi Arabia, see [If a relative or friend offers you a visa, item 5.6: Let the agency carry the file, in this order](#if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa-item-5)): apply at BMET's emigration branch, 89/2 Kakrail, Dhaka-1000, or the Chattogram DEMO, Sarkari Karjo Bhaban-2, Agrabad (+8802333321639, democtg76@gmail.com) <sup>[1](#o1), [37](#o37), [76](#o76)</sup>. It takes 3 working days <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>. You'll need <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>:

- an application;
- the visa copy;
- any contract;
- your attested registration card;
- an affidavit on Tk 300 non-judicial stamp paper;
- the Welfare Board receipt.

If it isn't done <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>:

1. Contact BMET's Director (Emigration), diremi@bmet.gov.bd, 01711-135901.
2. Then the Additional Director General (Employment), adgemp@bmet.gov.bd, 02-48321949; in Chattogram, 01913631797.

> **Need to know**
> - What is BMET's own document list for the PDO?
> - How does a worker applying alone pay the welfare fee and premium?
> - Is the Tk 250 clearance or smart card fee still charged <sup>[1](#o1), [N125 (2024)](#n125)</sup>?

<a id="before-you-fly"></a>

#### 2.9.3 Before you fly

1. <a id="before-you-fly-item-1"></a>**Check the ticket** under Manage My Booking on the airline's website with the PNR or e-ticket number and your last name; on Saudia it shows the booking if it exists <sup>[N126](#n126)</sup>. A seat held without your name and passport copy isn't a real booking <sup>[N127](#n127)</sup>; fares were about Tk 48,000-50,000 in May 2025 <sup>[N127](#n127)</sup>.
2. <a id="before-you-fly-item-2"></a>**Leave one set of copies of your papers at home and carry at least 3** <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>.
3. <a id="before-you-fly-item-3"></a>**Open at least two bank accounts,** yours and a family member's <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>.
4. <a id="before-you-fly-item-4"></a>**Carry no packet for anyone** if you don't know what's inside (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>.
5. <a id="before-you-fly-item-5"></a>**Ask for the worker fare:** with a BMET smart card, a new worker gets a base fare of USD 360 plus taxes on Dhaka-Jeddah, Dhaka-Madinah and Dhaka-Riyadh flights <sup>[N92](#n92)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does the fare still apply?
   > - Where do you complain if an airline or agency refuses it?

<a id="scams"></a>

### 2.10 Scams

Done to workers:

- **Free visa:** see [Avoid a "free visa"](#avoid-a-free-visa).
- **Shell-company visas:** visas in the names of companies that don't exist; the broker vanishes once the worker lands <sup>[N128 (2024)](#n128)</sup>. Check the company (see [Step 1, item 5: Check the company exists](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-5)).
- **Wrong visa or job:** brokers promise a 15-month iqama and send the worker on a 3-month tourist visa, after which the worker is illegal <sup>[N128 (2024)](#n128)</sup>; others promise one job and deliver another with a 3-month iqama <sup>[N128 (2024)](#n128)</sup>. Check the visa type and job title yourself before paying (see [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself)).
- **Switched contract:** the contract handed over at the airport, or the one on arrival, can name a different employer, job or wage <sup>[N3](#n3)</sup>. Compare it with the attested contract (see [Step 2: Get the attested demand letter](#step-2-get-the-attested-demand-letter)) and, after arrival, with your Qiwa contract, which is the one that counts ([After you arrive](#after-you-arrive)) <sup>[37](#o37), [N100](#n100)</sup>; being sent to a different job is grounds for a BMET complaint (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)) <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Huroob removal for a fee** (see ["If your employer reports you absent (huroob)"](#if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob)) <sup>[N101](#n101)</sup>, and **requests for your Absher or Qiwa codes,** which let someone act in your name <sup>[N40 (2024)](#n40)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - How does a worker check which phone numbers are registered under their iqama, and cancel ones they didn't open?

<a id="avoid-a-free-visa"></a>

#### 2.10.1 Avoid a "free visa"

There is no such thing as a free visa <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>: it is a visa bought from a sponsor with no job for you, so you must find your own work and housing, often pay the sponsor a monthly cut and renewal money (see ["What you pay, and what you earn and keep"](#what-you-pay-and-what-you-earn-and-keep), On a free visa you pay it yourself), work illegally and can be deported <sup>[N3](#n3), [N23 (2020)](#n23), [N33 (2024)](#n33)</sup>. Signs: the contract signed in Bangladesh isn't the one used in Saudi Arabia <sup>[N23 (2020)](#n23)</sup>, and the signs given for a relative's visa (see [If a relative or friend offers you a visa](#if-a-relative-or-friend-offers-you-a-visa), items 1 and 7): a household sponsor, a 3-month iqama. Have the papers checked (see [Before you pay anyone](#before-you-pay-anyone)) <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>; report the seller to BMET or the ministry (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)) or on the ministry hotline 16135 (+880 9610102030 from abroad) <sup>[1](#o1), [69](#o69), [37](#o37)</sup>; in an emergency call 999, the police emergency number <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>.

<a id="after-you-arrive"></a>

### 2.11 After you arrive

<a id="residence-card-iqama-and-medical"></a>

#### 2.11.1 Residence card (iqama) and medical

You enter on a 3-month permit; within it the employer must get your iqama and work permit (about SR 11,000 a year) <sup>[N61 (2024)](#n61)</sup>, after your medical test and its health insurance for you <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>. Otherwise you become undocumented and can be detained and deported <sup>[N61 (2024)](#n61)</sup>. The employer pays for it and its renewals <sup>[19](#o19), [30](#o30)</sup>; you do the medical test and fingerprints, and it takes 1-3 weeks once papers are complete <sup>[N129](#n129)</sup>. Check the name matches your passport; the employer corrects it at the Passports Directorate (Jawazat) <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>. Carry it always and show it to police on request <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>. Without it you can't open a bank account, use an ATM, send money or get a SIM <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.

*No iqama after 90 days:* complain to the labour ministry on 19911 (Sunday to Thursday, 8 am to 5 pm) <sup>[19](#o19), [77](#o77)</sup>; then take the steps for unpaid wages (see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid)) and call the embassy labour wing on 8001000125 <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.

**Set up Absher,** the Interior Ministry's portal for your iqama, visas and appointments <sup>[79](#o79)</sup>.

**You'll need**
<sup>[N43](#n43)</sup>:

- your iqama number;
- a Saudi mobile number.

1. On [absher.sa](https://www.absher.sa/) press New User, enter your iqama number, a password and the SMS code <sup>[N43](#n43)</sup>.
2. Activate it with Nafath, the Saudi digital ID app <sup>[N43](#n43)</sup>, or at an Absher kiosk in a mall or Jawazat office (iqama number, both index fingers, mobile number, code) <sup>[N43](#n43)</sup>.
3. If that fails, book an appointment on Absher and activate it free at a Jawazat office <sup>[N43](#n43)</sup>.

Support: 920020405 <sup>[N43](#n43)</sup>.

**Check your contract on Qiwa,** the labour ministry's platform, where every contract must be recorded <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

**You'll need**
<sup>[N100](#n100)</sup>:

- your iqama number;
- your date of birth as on the iqama;
- Nafath.

1. Register at [sso.qiwa.sa](https://sso.qiwa.sa/en/registration) and confirm your identity with Nafath <sup>[N100](#n100)</sup>.
2. The employer sends the contract to your account; what you approve there is the record of your pay and terms, so understand every term before approving and keep a copy <sup>[N100](#n100), [10](#o10)</sup>.

Support: 920000105, support@qiwa.sa <sup>[N100](#n100)</sup>.

<a id="welfare-board-sign-up-if-you-left-without-bmet-clearance"></a>

#### 2.11.2 Welfare Board sign-up if you left without BMET clearance

**If you came without BMET clearance,** join the Welfare Board from Saudi Arabia to get its insurance and help: apply at [portal.wewb.gov.bd](https://portal.wewb.gov.bd/) with your passport and proof you work legally; the embassy labour wing processes it and you pay the Tk 5,500 welfare fee in riyals <sup>[80](#o80), [36](#o36)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How much in riyals does the labour wing now charge for Welfare Board membership?

<a id="your-rights-at-work"></a>

### 2.12 Your rights at work

If a right is broken: see [Where to complain](#where-to-complain).

<a id="contract"></a>

#### 2.12.1 Contract

Your contract must be recorded on Qiwa ([After you arrive](#after-you-arrive)), with a copy for you and the employer <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. It runs for a fixed term, usually 2 years; with no length written it counts as one year, and it renews for the same length if both sides carry on <sup>[22](#o22), [10](#o10)</sup>. It should state your leave, whether it is paid and who pays the airfare <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.

**Probation**
Probation can last up to 180 days if the contract sets it; either side can end the contract during it without compensation or end-of-service pay <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. Who pays the ticket home: see [If you resign or are dismissed](#if-you-resign-or-are-dismissed).

<a id="pay-and-deductions"></a>

#### 2.12.2 Pay and deductions

There is no minimum wage for foreign workers; the contract wage is what you can claim <sup>[N40 (2024)](#n40)</sup>. It is paid into your bank account on the contract date <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. Never sign that you were paid before the money arrives; it can block a later claim <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. Recruitment, iqama and work permit fees and renewals, job-title changes, exit and re-entry visas, transfer fees and your ticket home at the contract's end are the employer's and can't be taken from your pay <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

The employer must pay your wages on time through the Wage Protection Program, which records each payment <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>, and give housing or a housing allowance <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>; none of the costs above may be deducted <sup>[30](#o30)</sup>. Typical monthly pay <sup>[N130](#n130)</sup>:

- helpers: SR 1,200-1,800;
- cleaners: SR 1,000-1,700;
- construction workers and masons: SR 1,600-3,000;
- drivers: SR 1,500-2,500;
- electricians: SR 1,800-3,500.

> **Need to know**
> - How can a worker see their recorded wage payments?

<a id="hours-rest-and-leave"></a>

#### 2.12.3 Hours, rest and leave

You work at most 8 hours a day and 48 a week (6 and 36 for Muslims in Ramadan), never over 12 a day in total <sup>[10](#o10), [22](#o22)</sup>, with a break of at least 30 minutes after 5 hours in a row <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. From 15 June to 15 September, no private-sector work in direct sun from 12 noon to 3 pm; report a breach on 19911 or in the labour ministry's app (see [Where to complain](#where-to-complain)) <sup>[81](#o81), [82](#o82), [83](#o83)</sup>. Heat stroke (confusion, slurred speech, fainting, seizures, very high temperature, hot dry or drenched skin) kills if treatment is delayed: get emergency care, move the person into shade, take off outer clothing and cool them with water <sup>[N131](#n131)</sup>. Headache, nausea, dizziness, weakness and heavy sweating are heat exhaustion: get out of the heat, drink and get checked at a clinic <sup>[N131](#n131)</sup>.

**Overtime**
Overtime pays your full hourly wage plus half the basic hourly wage per extra hour; public-holiday work counts as overtime <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

**Rest and leave**
- *Weekly rest:* one day off a week, normally Friday, not swappable for money <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.
- *Annual leave:* paid, at least 21 days, 30 after 5 years with the same employer, on dates the employer sets with 30 days' notice; untaken days are paid when you leave <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.
- *Paid holidays:* 4 days at each Eid, Founding Day (22 February), National Day (23 September) <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.
- *Sick leave:* up to 120 days a year, 30 at full pay, 60 at three-quarters, 30 unpaid <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.
- *Other paid leave:* 3 days for a child's birth, 5 for your wedding, 5 for the death of a spouse, parent or child, 10 to 15 days once for Hajj after 2 years; 12 weeks' maternity leave <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

<a id="housing-food-and-health-care"></a>

#### 2.12.4 Housing, food and health care

The employer gives housing and transport or allowances for them <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>; food only if the contract says so <sup>[22](#o22)</sup>.

**Health insurance**
The employer must insure you <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. Check your cover with the Council of Health Insurance's Insurance Information Inquiry at [chi.gov.sa](https://www.chi.gov.sa/en/ServicesDirectory/Pages/Eservices-CheckInsurance.aspx) <sup>[84](#o84)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which number does the check ask for (iqama or passport)?
> - What does the result show?

If you aren't insured or the premium is taken from your pay, complain at [care.chi.gov.sa](https://care.chi.gov.sa/) (guest or account) with your details, the employer's contact details, what happened and scanned proof; it is handled within 15 working days without naming you to the employer <sup>[85 (2024)](#o85)</sup>. The cover doesn't pay for serious illness, so go only if you are fit <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.

<a id="your-passport-1"></a>

#### 2.12.5 Your passport

No employer may keep your passport or iqama; the fine is SR 5,000, though sponsors still commonly do it <sup>[10](#o10), [N40 (2024)](#n40)</sup>. Complain on the labour ministry's Beneficiary Page ([selfservice.hrsd.gov.sa](https://selfservice.hrsd.gov.sa/node/)) <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>: [hrsd.gov.sa](https://www.hrsd.gov.sa/en/ministry-services/services/client-page) > Contact Us > Beneficiary Page > Start the Service > log in with Nafath > Complaints > Add a Request; choose the sector and categories, fill in the fields, press Send <sup>[86 (2024)](#o86)</sup>. It needs a ministry or Absher account, is free, answered within 5 days, and also in the ministry's app <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>.

Holding them "for safekeeping" is not allowed either <sup>[10](#o10), [19](#o19)</sup>. Besides the Beneficiary Page, you can call 19911 <sup>[77](#o77)</sup>.

<a id="your-residence-permit"></a>

### 2.13 Your residence permit

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay"></a>

#### 2.13.1 How long you can stay

You can stay as long as you have a contract and a valid iqama, which the employer renews and pays for <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. Work visas are for ages 18 to 60 <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>. **No pension:** GOSI, the Saudi social insurance agency, covers foreign workers only for work injuries <sup>[88](#o88)</sup>; you get end-of-service pay instead ([Leaving at the end of the job](#leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job)) <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

<a id="renewing-your-passport-and-nid"></a>

#### 2.13.2 Renewing your passport and NID

**Passport.** An exit and re-entry visa needs your passport valid 90 days, a final exit 60 days <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>.

1. Book "E-Passport Service" on [passportriyadh.com/appointment](https://passportriyadh.com/appointment/), at the embassy in Riyadh or another city, with your name, phone and passport number <sup>[90](#o90)</sup>.
2. Fill in the form online <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>.
3. Bring your old passport and your NID or an online birth registration certificate in English <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>.

A worker's 48-page, 5-year e-passport costs SR 125 (SR 190 urgent), a 10-year one SR 210 <sup>[N132 (2024)](#n132)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does it take?
> - How do you pay?
> - What is the Jeddah consulate's booking route?

**NID.** If you are over 16 and never had one, fill in the form at [services.nidw.gov.bd](https://services.nidw.gov.bd/nid-pub/), then on the appointment date hand in the documents at the mission and give a photo, fingerprints, iris scan and signature <sup>[92](#o92)</sup>; missions in Saudi Arabia had begun NID registration by July 2025 <sup>[N133](#n133)</sup>. A mission can't correct an existing NID <sup>[92](#o92)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How do you book NID registration at the Riyadh embassy or Jeddah consulate?
> - How does a worker abroad correct an NID that doesn't match the passport?

<a id="going-home-on-leave-and-coming-back"></a>

#### 2.13.3 Going home on leave and coming back

Apply for an exit and re-entry visa yourself on Absher; the employer is notified, its approval isn't needed, and it pays the fee <sup>[24](#o24), [10](#o10)</sup>:

- SR 200 for one trip up to 2 months;
- SR 100 per extra month;
- SR 500 for multiple trips over 3 months <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27), [N134](#n134)</sup>.

You must be in Saudi Arabia, with <sup>[89](#o89), [N52](#n52)</sup>:

- a passport valid at least 90 days;
- fingerprints registered;
- no unpaid traffic or other fines;
- a valid iqama and Qiwa contract;
- for a trip set in months, an iqama covering 90 days plus the trip.

"Return Before" on the visa is your last day to come back <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>. Your contract says who pays the ticket <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>. At the airport in Bangladesh, the Probashi Kallyan (expatriate welfare) desk checks the re-entry visa <sup>[N135](#n135)</sup>. On Absher you declare you understand the measures taken if you don't return before your approved leave ends <sup>[N52](#n52)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are those measures, now that the old three-year entry ban is lifted <sup>[N136 (2024)](#n136)</sup>?

<a id="bringing-your-family"></a>

#### 2.13.4 Bringing your family

Skilled workers may bring a spouse, sons under 25 and unmarried daughters ([Family residence](#family-residence)) <sup>[93](#o93)</sup>. Each work permit is graded High-skilled, Skilled or Basic on the employer's Qiwa account; you can ask for a wrong grade to be corrected, which depends on the job title in your Qiwa contract being right <sup>[58](#o58)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a worker see their own grade?
> - Does High-skilled count as skilled here?

<a id="what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported"></a>

### 2.14 What can get you jailed or deported

- **Don't bring** narcotic drugs, alcohol, anything with pork, poppy seeds (khas khas), khat, gutkha, pan masala, non-Islamic religious literature, obscene material, amulets or anything linked to black magic, including on your phone <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.
- **Never carry a parcel you haven't packed and checked yourself,** even for family <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>; Saudi Arabia executes foreigners for drug smuggling <sup>[N137](#n137)</sup>.
- **Medicines:** your own only, at most 30 days' supply, in original packs, with a medical report and a prescription under 6 months old, both translated into Arabic <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.
- **Don't work for anyone but the employer on your permit, or in another job,** even for a few hours <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.
- **Cybercrime** cases have brought Bangladeshis 6 months to 5 years in prison <sup>[N138](#n138)</sup>; in October 2026 a Bangladeshi man and woman, held under the cybercrime law and on an adultery allegation, were executed for an "indecent act" in a mosque <sup>[N139](#n139), [N138](#n138)</sup>.

<a id="driving"></a>

#### 2.14.1 Driving

If you cause a crash that kills someone, the family can claim blood money (diya): a Riyadh court ordered an uninsured foreign driver to pay SR 300,000 per person killed and kept him in prison until he paid <sup>[N140 (2012)](#n140)</sup>. Compulsory motor insurance pays victims up to SR 10 million, but the insurer can take that money back from a driver without the right class of licence or with an expired one <sup>[94 (2020)](#o94)</sup>.

<a id="if-you-or-a-relative-is-arrested"></a>

#### 2.14.2 If you or a relative is arrested

The court provides an interpreter, but you can't choose your own <sup>[N138](#n138)</sup>. Saudi authorities may not tell the embassy about a death sentence, even one carried out <sup>[N138](#n138)</sup>. A relative or friend reports it to the labour wing on its online form, choosing the jail problem type, or on 8001000125, and asks the mission for legal help, which no family of the two executed in 2026 had done <sup>[59](#o59), [78](#o78), [N138](#n138)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What legal help does the embassy give in practice (lawyer, visits)?
> - How does a family in Bangladesh find out where a relative is held?

<a id="if-something-goes-wrong"></a>

### 2.15 If something goes wrong

<a id="if-you-arent-paid"></a>

#### 2.15.1 If you aren't paid

You can claim unpaid wages, overtime, leave pay and end-of-service pay. Act in this order.

1. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-1"></a>**Collect proof:** your Qiwa contract, bank statements, payslips, messages about pay, and a list of missing months and amounts <sup>[N141](#n141), [10](#o10)</sup>. Sign nothing saying you were paid <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

2. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-2"></a>**File a friendly-settlement claim with the labour ministry.** Every wage claim starts here; unsettled claims go to the labour court <sup>[10](#o10), [27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>.
   You'll need <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27), [95 (2024)](#o95), [96](#o96)</sup>:

   - your iqama number;
   - a phone for SMS codes (a Bangladeshi number can be added);
   - the employer's name, your job title and start date;
   - the city where you last worked;
   - each claim with months and amounts;
   - proof as PDF, JPG, JPEG, TIF or PNG files up to 5 MB each.

   1. On the [Friendly Settlement service page](https://www.hrsd.gov.sa/en/ministry-services/services/269970) press Start Service and log in to the Individual Portal <sup>[96](#o96), [27 (2024)](#o27), [95 (2024)](#o95)</sup>.
   2. Choose Amicable Settlement Lawsuit Filing, check your details, pick your employer (or Another Establishment to type it), enter the job details and the settlement office for your city, add each claim with its amount, attach proof, tick the declaration and press Submit Claim <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27), [96](#o96)</sup>. Keep it short, with dates, amounts and a list of attachments <sup>[N141](#n141)</sup>. Template: "I worked for [company] as [job] from [date]. My contract wage is SR [amount] a month. I have not been paid for [months], a total of SR [amount]. I ask for these wages to be paid. Attached: contract, bank statements, messages." You get a claim number at once and the review result by SMS within 48 hours <sup>[95 (2024)](#o95)</sup>. File within 12 months; settlement is meant to take 21 working days <sup>[96](#o96), [27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>.
   3. Join the session by Webex video call and show your iqama; there is no interpreter for non-Arabic speakers <sup>[95 (2024)](#o95)</sup>. The settlement service is offered in Arabic, English and Urdu <sup>[96](#o96)</sup>. If you miss it the claim is dismissed, but you can reopen it from My Claims within 21 working days <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27), [95 (2024)](#o95)</sup>. If the employer doesn't come, its services are suspended and a new date is set; if it keeps not coming, you can move employer without its consent and the claim goes to court <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>. An attorney, including an embassy attorney, can file the claim for you with a power of attorney number <sup>[95 (2024)](#o95)</sup>.
   4. If settled, print the settlement report from My Lawsuits <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>; if not, the claim goes to the labour court on Najiz, the Justice Ministry's online court system <sup>[10](#o10), [27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How does a foreign worker first create the Individual Portal login (Nafath or Absher)?
   > - Does filing cost SR 0 or SR 100 <sup>[96](#o96), [27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>?
   > - May an interpreter or the labour wing join the session?
   > - How do you enforce a settlement the employer ignores?

3. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-3"></a>**At the same time, ask the Bangladesh Embassy's labour wing for help.** It advises workers in disputes and goes to the labour court with them <sup>[62](#o62)</sup>. Call free on 8001000125 in Saudi Arabia or 0114195300 (extensions 401-403), or email counselorriyadh@probashi.gov.bd or labourwing.riyadh@yahoo.com <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>. Or use its [online service request form](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/): name, mobile, city, iqama and passport numbers, the problem type (for wages, the housing, food and wage one), a description in Bangla or English, and copies of your iqama and passport <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>. Template: "I am [name], iqama [number], working for [company] in [city]. I have not been paid since [month]. I have filed a friendly-settlement claim, number [number]. Please help me with the hearing." Office: 8039 Dareen Street, Diplomatic Quarter, Riyadh <sup>[62](#o62)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What are its office hours?
   > - What do you bring to a walk-in visit?
   > - How soon does it answer the form?
   > - How do you give it a power of attorney?

4. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-4"></a>**From Bangladesh, a relative can ask the government to act.** Send the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare (Welfare-1 branch, saswelfare@probashi.gov.bd, 02-41030251) a short application saying what happened and the wages, allowances or insurance owed, with <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>:

   - copies of your passport, iqama and BMET smart card;
   - your Saudi ID number;
   - the employer's name and full address.

   It is free, and within 7 working days the ministry asks the labour wing and the Wage Earners' Welfare Board to act <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>. Template: "My [brother], [name], passport [number], works for [company], [address], Saudi Arabia. He has not been paid since [month]; SR [amount] is owed. Copies attached. Please ask the labour wing in Riyadh to recover his wages." If nothing happens, contact the head of its Mission and Welfare wing: 02-41030219, 01711427769, adsmission@probashi.gov.bd <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>. OKUP (01842773300) gives free legal advice and helps families take cases <sup>[N142](#n142)</sup>.

5. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-5"></a>**If the whole company has stopped paying, claim wage insurance.** It pays only when the employer hasn't paid 80% or more of its workers for over 6 months, after friendly settlement <sup>[97 (2024)](#o97)</sup>: up to 6 months' unpaid wages at your Qiwa contract rate and up to SR 1,000 for a ticket home, at most SR 17,500, not end-of-service pay <sup>[97 (2024)](#o97)</sup>. You can claim in Saudi Arabia, even after changing employer <sup>[97 (2024)](#o97)</sup>; domestic, seasonal, temporary and farm workers aren't covered <sup>[97 (2024)](#o97)</sup>.

   You'll need <sup>[97 (2024)](#o97)</sup>:

   - the claim form and declaration;
   - proof of the last wage received;
   - your contract;
   - the official document showing wages owed;
   - your iqama copy;
   - your final exit visa, for the ticket.

   1. Check your cover with your iqama number at [labor.aletihad.sa](https://labor.aletihad.sa/), run by the insurer Al-Etihad Cooperative Insurance (free phone 800 3040 110) <sup>[97 (2024)](#o97), [98](#o98)</sup>.
   2. Claim in its Worker Portal (Submit Claim) with your iqama or border number and mobile, saying whether you have left Saudi Arabia <sup>[98](#o98)</sup>.
   3. You or a legal representative send the documents listed under You'll need above <sup>[97 (2024)](#o97)</sup>.
   4. The employer has 10 working days to object <sup>[97 (2024)](#o97)</sup>.

   Most wage-theft cases fall outside these rules, and victims are rarely paid, sometimes only after years <sup>[N143](#n143)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What do you do if the claim is refused?

6. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-item-6"></a>**After 3 months without pay you may change employer without consent** (see [If you want to change employer](#if-you-want-to-change-employer)) <sup>[N52](#n52)</sup>.

<a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob"></a>

#### 2.15.2 If your employer reports you absent (huroob)

Huroob ("running away") is an employer's Qiwa report that you stopped coming to work: your status becomes "Absent from Work", it can't be withdrawn, and you are removed from the company's records <sup>[26 (2022)](#o26)</sup>. It can be filed only if you have no contract recorded on Qiwa, your permit or iqama has at least 60 days left, and you have been absent over 30 days in a contract year or 15 in a row <sup>[26 (2022)](#o26), [N144](#n144)</sup>, so make sure your contract is on Qiwa. When a Qiwa contract ends you get 60 days to move, leave or sign again before being marked absent <sup>[N144](#n144), [N145](#n145)</sup>.

1. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob-item-1"></a>**Check your status often** on your Absher dashboard ("Absent from Work" or blocked services) <sup>[N101](#n101)</sup> or, with your iqama number or passport number and nationality, on the ministry's [expatriate employee inquiry](https://es.hrsd.gov.sa/services/inquiry/nonsaudiempinquiry.aspx) <sup>[99](#o99)</sup>. The SMS alert may not arrive if your number isn't linked to the government system <sup>[26 (2022)](#o26), [N40 (2024)](#n40)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob-item-2"></a>**Within 60 days, move or leave:** transfer on Qiwa if you have been in Saudi Arabia over 12 months, or issue yourself a final exit visa on Absher and go home; otherwise you stay "absent" and bear the penalties <sup>[26 (2022)](#o26)</sup>. A final exit visa needs <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>:

   - you in Saudi Arabia;
   - a valid iqama;
   - a passport valid at least 60 days;
   - traffic fines paid;
   - fingerprints registered;
   - no vehicle in your name.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the Absher menu path for a worker's own final exit visa?
   > - What is the fee for a worker's own final exit visa?
   > - How long is a worker's own final exit visa valid?

3. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob-item-3"></a>**After 60 days,** if you have been in Saudi Arabia over 12 months, a new employer can still move you on Qiwa, paying overdue permit fees <sup>[N145](#n145), [N146](#n146)</sup>; otherwise apply to the embassy's Special Exit Programme ([Going home without valid papers](#going-home-without-valid-papers)), which excludes people with a police or court case <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob-item-4"></a>**If the report is false:** employers file false huroob reports to exploit workers; the burden of proof is on you, and some workers are asked for bribes to withdraw it <sup>[N60 (2022)](#n60)</sup>. Tell the labour wing on its online form (see [If you aren't paid, item 3: At the same time, ask the Bangladesh Embassy's labour wing for help](#if-you-arent-paid-item-3)), choosing the huroob problem type, or the court or jail type if you are also accused of a crime <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Can friendly settlement or the labour court cancel a false report?
   > - How do you answer a false theft charge?

Anyone offering to remove a huroob for a fee is a scammer; a real change shows only on Absher, Qiwa or the ministry's site <sup>[N101](#n101)</sup>.

<a id="where-to-complain"></a>

#### 2.15.3 Where to complain

**If a right is broken,** report it in the labour ministry's (Ministry of Human Resources and Social Development) app, on Google Play and the App Store, or in Tawakkalna, the Saudi government app: service Reporting Violations of Labor Regulations, sign in with Absher, pick the report type, give the workplace location, company details and proof; it is free, the ministry acts within 3 days and the status shows in the app <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>. Money owed is claimed through friendly settlement ([If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid)) <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. Ministry help line: 19911, Sunday to Thursday 8 am to 5 pm, Arabic and English <sup>[100 (2024)](#o100)</sup>.

<a id="if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money"></a>

#### 2.15.4 If an agency, broker or relative takes your money

**If the agency takes your money and doesn't send you, sends you to a different job or overcharges, complain to BMET.** It is free and meant to take 30 working days: an inquiry committee hears both sides, BMET recommends, the ministry decides <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>. In practice many complaints stay unresolved for years <sup>[N110 (2024)](#n110)</sup>.

You'll need:

- your name, NID number, passport number, BMET smart card number if you have one, age, father's or husband's and mother's names, permanent address and phone <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>;
- if abroad, your address there and a relative (name, relation, mobile) to attend for you <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>;
- the agency's name, RL number and address, or the broker's name, age, parents' names and address <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>;
- any earlier complaints against them <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>;
- proof such as receipts and the contract <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.

1. <a id="if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money-item-1"></a>**File online** on OEP's complaint system (BMET's "online complaint" link goes to employee.oep.gov.bd); you or your family can upload documents and follow progress <sup>[N75](#n75), [101](#o101)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money-item-2"></a>**Or on paper:** fill in BMET's [complaint form (Form Ka)](https://objectstorage.ap-dcc-gazipur-1.oraclecloud15.com/n/axvjbnqprylg/b/V2Ministry/o/office-bmet/2024/12/fe647f3d68c14792a15f496990376a8a.PDF) with the reason and what happened, sign or thumbprint it, and hand it with copies of your proof to BMET (89/2 Kakrail, Dhaka-1000), your DEMO, or the Bangladesh Embassy's labour wing if abroad <sup>[49](#o49), [37](#o37)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money-item-3"></a>**No action in 30 working days:** write to BMET's complaint officer, the Director (Employment), diremi@bmet.gov.bd, 01711111544, who has 3 months <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money-item-4"></a>**Then** appeal to the Secretary, Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare, secretary@probashi.gov.bd, 02-41030444 (+880 2 41030444 from abroad), who has 1 month; then [grs.gov.bd](https://www.grs.gov.bd/), 3 months <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.
5. <a id="if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money-item-5"></a>**Or go to the ministry directly:** its Enforcement-2 branch, sasenforcement@probashi.gov.bd or 02-41030255, with an application, proof, your full address and the agency's name, RL number and address; free, action within 3-5 working days, settlement meant within 30 working days <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>. Hotline 16135, or +880 9610102030 from abroad <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>.

**Free legal help:** OKUP (Ovibashi Karmi Unnayan Program, a migrant workers' organisation), 01842773300, gives legal advice and helps file a case <sup>[N142](#n142)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the menu path to OEP's complaint form?
> - Does a paper complaint get a receipt or complaint number?
> - How do you follow up a paper complaint?
> - Does IOM in Dhaka take up individual complaints (the ILO doesn't <sup>[102](#o102)</sup>)?

**If the relative cheats you.** The person most likely to cheat you is someone you know: in a survey of 5,400 households in Kalihati, Tangail, 40% of migrant households had been defrauded and 19% had paid and never gone, mostly by relatives, friends or neighbours with connections, which is why victims avoid the courts <sup>[N109 (2019)](#n109)</sup>. The law reaches the relative. Section 31 of the Overseas Employment and Migrants Act 2013 (OEMA) makes it a crime for any person, not only an agency, to take money with a false promise of a job abroad or to keep a migrant's passport or visa: up to 5 years and a fine of at least Tk 1 lakh <sup>[103 (2013)](#o103)</sup>. These offences are cognisable, non-bailable and can't be settled privately <sup>[104 (2013)](#o104)</sup>. But section 33 makes buying or selling a visa inside Bangladesh an offence for any person, 2 to 7 years' rigorous imprisonment and at least Tk 3 lakh, so a worker who paid for a visa can be accused too, and the seller will say so <sup>[105](#o105)</sup>. Frame the complaint as money taken on a false promise of a job (section 31), and get legal advice before going to the thana. All of these routes can run at once:

- **Free legal help first.** OEMA gives people cheated in the name of migration a right to legal aid <sup>[106 (2013)](#o106)</sup>. Call 16430, free in office hours (from abroad +880 9123 16430), or apply at the District Legal Aid Office in the district judge court building, which also mediates fraud and money disputes free for people earning up to Tk 1 lakh a year <sup>[N147 (2023)](#n147)</sup>. OKUP, 01842773300, helps families file cases <sup>[N142](#n142)</sup>.
- **Name the people in Bangladesh who took the money.** When the relative lives in Saudi Arabia, his family at home usually received the payment, as with the Banshkhali man's wife and the Bishwanath family <sup>[N99](#n99), [N94](#n94)</sup>. Complain against each person named on the bank and bKash records, not only the man abroad <sup>[N99](#n99)</sup>.
- **Village salish (সালিস) or the Union Parishad chairman.** Most families go here first, and it rarely returns the money: in Natore a salish ordered the man to repay Tk 1 lakh to each family, a fraction of what they paid, and he ignored it; in Bishwanath the accused ignored three notices, and the chairman certified the victims to go to court <sup>[N103 (2023)](#n103), [N94](#n94)</sup>. BRAC's migration programme runs social arbitration instead, where volunteers and local leaders press the broker to repay <sup>[N148](#n148)</sup>.
- **Village court (গ্রাম আদালত)** at the Union Parishad, for money owed under an agreement, receipt or other document, up to Tk 3 lakh since 2024: apply on Form 1 with a Tk 20 fee <sup>[107 (2024)](#o107), [N149](#n149)</sup>.
- **Complain to BMET or the ministry against the relative by name.** Section 41 lets anyone harmed complain against any person, not only an agency; the inquiry is meant to take 30 working days, and section 43 lets the government recover the money and pay it to you <sup>[108 (2013)](#o108), [109 (2013)](#o109)</sup>. Use the agency complaint route (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)), giving the person's name, age, parents' names and address <sup>[49](#o49)</sup>. BMET settled 2,054 complaints and recovered nearly Tk 9.92 crore in 2025 <sup>[N150](#n150)</sup>, but it pays on the official cost of the job, the legal maximum of Tk 1.65 lakh (see [The legal maximum and what people really pay](#the-legal-maximum-and-what-people-really-pay)), rather than what you paid, and can't enforce its decision <sup>[N3](#n3), [N109 (2019)](#n109)</sup>.
- **Police case (FIR) at the thana** under OEMA section 31, or under the human trafficking law if the person was sold, confined or forced to work <sup>[N108](#n108)</sup>. Expect resistance: in Natore the officer in charge refused nine complaints, saying the men hadn't gone abroad legally so the money wasn't the police's job <sup>[N103 (2023)](#n103)</sup>.
- **If the police won't register it, file a complaint case (সি.আর. মামলা, CR case) at the magistrate's court,** as the Bishwanath victims did at the Senior Judicial Magistrate's court in Sylhet <sup>[N94](#n94)</sup>. A first-class judicial magistrate tries OEMA offences, and the trial is meant to end within 4 months of charges, extendable by 2 <sup>[110 (2013)](#o110)</sup>. You can sue for compensation in a civil court alongside <sup>[111 (2013)](#o111)</sup>.

**If the relative lives in Saudi Arabia,** a case against him alone stalls: a Cumilla broker's case against the friend in Saudi Arabia who supplied his visas went nowhere, and in Natore the salish happened only after the man came home and the families went to his house <sup>[N33 (2024)](#n33), [N103 (2023)](#n103)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has any family recovered visa money from a relative through BRAC's arbitration, a village court, a police case or BMET?
> - If so, how much did they recover and how long did it take?
> - Does the labour wing in Riyadh or Jeddah act on a complaint against a Bangladeshi in Saudi Arabia who took visa or iqama money?

<a id="changing-employer-or-leaving"></a>

### 2.16 Changing employer or leaving

<a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer"></a>

#### 2.16.1 If you want to change employer

**You can move without your employer's consent:**

- when your Qiwa contract has ended <sup>[23 (2020)](#o23)</sup>;
- after 12 months' work since entering Saudi Arabia, with 90 days' notice (less if the employer agrees) and a valid contract on Qiwa <sup>[23 (2020)](#o23), [N52](#n52)</sup>;
- at any time if you haven't been paid for 3 months in a row, your work permit or iqama has expired, the employer has disappeared (travel, prison, death), or you report that the business is secretly run by a foreigner in a Saudi's name and you weren't part of it <sup>[N52](#n52)</sup>;
- if the employer keeps missing your friendly-settlement sessions <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>.

Your employer is only notified and can't refuse <sup>[N52](#n52)</sup>.

1. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-1"></a>**Find a new employer yourself;** workers go to industrial areas and apply at every factory <sup>[N52](#n52)</sup>. Check the company (see [Step 1, item 5: Check the company exists](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-5)). Pay no one for a job offer or transfer; the new employer pays the fees <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does any official service match foreign workers already in Saudi Arabia with employers?

2. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-2"></a>**Accept its offer on Qiwa:** log in with Nafath, open the offer, check the job title and wage, accept or reject <sup>[N151](#n151), [112](#o112)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-3"></a>**Your employer is notified and your notice runs,** unless waived <sup>[112](#o112), [N52](#n52)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-4"></a>**The new employer completes the move;** once approved, the new work permit is issued at once, at no cost to you <sup>[112](#o112), [10](#o10), [N52](#n52)</sup>.

Keep working for your current employer until Qiwa shows the move is done, or you may never get a permit and stay illegal <sup>[N40 (2024)](#n40)</sup>. Never share your Absher or Qiwa password or SMS codes, even with your employer <sup>[N40 (2024)](#n40)</sup>. Some employers block moves by cancelling the iqama with a final exit visa or accusing the worker of theft or debt, and some new employers wrongly ask for the old employer's approval <sup>[N40 (2024)](#n40), [N52](#n52)</sup>; if blocked, file friendly settlement <sup>[N146](#n146)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What do you do if the transfer is rejected or never completed?

<a id="if-you-resign-or-are-dismissed"></a>

#### 2.16.2 If you resign or are dismissed

**Notice.** Give notice 30 days before a fixed-term contract ends, or 60 days on an open-ended one <sup>[22](#o22)</sup>. A resignation counts as accepted after 30 days without an answer; the employer can delay it up to 60 days, and you can withdraw it within 7 <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

**If the employer dismisses you.** If the employer ends the contract without a valid reason, you are owed 15 days' wages per year of service on an open-ended contract or the wages for the rest of a fixed-term one, and never less than 2 months' wages <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

**What you pay if you leave early.** Since 2025 the law bars an employer's compensation claim against a worker who resigns <sup>[N51 (2024)](#n51)</sup>. The employer pays your ticket home, also when it ends the contract in a trial period; you pay it only if you are unfit for the work or quit without a valid reason <sup>[30](#o30), [10](#o10)</sup>. For what you are still owed, see [Leaving at the end of the job, item 3: Collect what you are owed](#leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-3).

> **Need to know**
> - Does an employer who ends the contract in the trial period owe the worker anything beyond the ticket home?

**Leaving without notice**
You may stop work without notice and keep all your rights if the employer doesn't meet its duties (for example, doesn't pay), moves you to another job without consent, assaults or mistreats you, leaves a known serious danger unfixed, or lied about the job when you signed <sup>[10](#o10), [52 (2023)](#o52)</sup>. If you just stop coming to work, the employer can report you absent (see ["If your employer reports you absent (huroob)"](#if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob)) <sup>[26 (2022)](#o26)</sup> and, after a written warning, dismiss you without end-of-service pay for over 30 days' absence in a contract year or 15 in a row <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.

<a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job"></a>

#### 2.16.3 Leaving at the end of the job

1. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-1"></a>**Give notice** (how much: see [If you resign or are dismissed](#if-you-resign-or-are-dismissed)).
2. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-2"></a>**Get your final exit visa yourself** on Absher or Qiwa, without the employer's consent; the employer is notified, and you bear the consequences of any breach of contract <sup>[24](#o24)</sup>. Conditions: see ["If your employer reports you absent (huroob)"](#if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob), item 2 <sup>[89](#o89)</sup>.
3. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-3"></a>**Collect what you are owed** within 1 week of the contract ending, or 2 if you ended it <sup>[52 (2023)](#o52)</sup>. End-of-service pay is half a month's last wage per year for the first 5 years and a full month per later year, part-years counted <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>; if you resign, you get a third after 2 to 5 years, two-thirds after 5 to 10, all after 10 <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. Untaken leave is paid <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>.
4. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-4"></a>**Get your service certificate,** free, with your dates, job and last wage, and your papers back <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. Or issue it yourself: qiwa.sa > Individual Account > Employment Certificates (Suggested Actions) > Apply Now > Service Certificate > select the job > Next Step > submit; it is approved at once for download <sup>[N144](#n144), [N152 (2024)](#n152)</sup>.
5. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-5"></a>**The employer pays your ticket home** <sup>[10](#o10)</sup>. If anything is unpaid, file friendly settlement within 12 months <sup>[96](#o96)</sup>.

**Check for a travel ban before you book**
An unpaid loan, credit card, rent or a cheque given as security can bring a court travel ban of up to 3 years per order <sup>[N153](#n153)</sup>. A ban shows in Absher's travel ban enquiry, and the debt case on Najiz, the Justice Ministry's portal (log in with Nafath) <sup>[N153](#n153)</sup>.

<a id="work-visa-domestic-worker"></a>

## 3 Work visa: domestic worker

This visa is for work in a Saudi household, in one of 14 listed domestic jobs such as housemaid, private driver, nanny, cook or gardener <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>. Domestic workers fall outside the Saudi Labour Law; the Domestic Workers Regulation sets their rights <sup>[25 (2024)](#o25), [113](#o113)</sup>. Musaned, the labour ministry's domestic-worker platform, handles contracts and pay <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6), [114](#o114)</sup>. The employer pays recruitment (up to SR 11,750 to the recruitment office for a Bangladeshi worker), the iqama, work permit and transfer fees <sup>[115 (2024)](#o115), [113](#o113)</sup>. A woman domestic worker pays nothing: her agency may take no migration cost from her <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>, and Saudi recruitment offices may take no commission from any domestic worker, before or after she starts work <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>. No skill test is needed <sup>[N8](#n8)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What may an agency lawfully charge a man going as a house driver or other male domestic worker?

<a id="step-1-check-you-qualify"></a>

### 3.1 Step 1: Check you qualify

- **Age.** A woman must be 25 to 45 for BMET clearance <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>; the housekeeping course (see [Step 3: Do the one-month housekeeping course (women)](#step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women)) takes women 25 to 45, returnees up to 50 <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>. Saudi law bans domestic workers under 21, and the contract normally ends at 60 <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.
- **Children, health, education, consent** <sup>[116](#o116), [1](#o1)</sup>:
  - A woman whose youngest child is under 6, who is pregnant, or who has a contagious or incurable disease can't join the course <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.
  - She needs class 5 (can be relaxed) and must read and write <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.
  - She needs a no-objection letter from her legal guardian for the course and BMET clearance <sup>[116](#o116), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Never let anyone change your age.** Agents forge a second birth certificate to get a passport for an under-age girl <sup>[N154 (2020)](#n154)</sup>. Check the birth certificate the passport will use at [everify.bdris.gov.bd](https://everify.bdris.gov.bd/), the birth registration office's check page, with the 17-digit birth registration number and the real date of birth <sup>[117](#o117)</sup>. If an agent offers to change the age, call the Probashi call centre on 16135 <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Which office takes a report of a forged birth certificate used for a passport?

<a id="step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency"></a>

### 3.2 Step 2: Find the job through an approved agency

A Saudi family picks a worker through a Saudi recruitment office on Musaned, which deals with the Bangladeshi agency; the visa goes to the Saudi embassy through the Foreign Ministry's platform <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6), [14](#o14)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency-item-1"></a>**Use only an agency the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare has approved to send women domestic workers.** Approval requires a Tk 15 lakh security with BMET, a woman on the office staff, and the licence at stake if a worker is abused <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>.
   1. Ask for the ministry's approval letter (memo number and date) and check the agency's name and RL (recruiting licence) number are in it <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>.
   2. Check the licence is active on OEP (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 1, item 4](#step-1-check-you-qualify)).

   Complaints about agencies: sasenforcement@probashi.gov.bd, 02-41030255 <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does the ministry publish one current list of all agencies approved to send women domestic workers?
   > - Where does the ministry post the approval letters?
   > - How can a worker confirm an approval letter is genuine?

2. <a id="step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency-item-2"></a>**Pay nothing.** Any request to a woman for money for the visa, ticket, training or "processing" breaks the approval conditions <sup>[2](#o2)</sup>; complain as for a company worker (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)).
3. <a id="step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency-item-3"></a>**Get the job in writing before leaving:** contract terms, type of work, workplace, wage and rest periods <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>; the wage is the one in the unified contract you both sign <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>. The Arabic text is official and must be translated into a language you understand; get the Bangla translation before signing <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>. If the agency won't show the contract or its Bangla translation, or the terms differ from what you were told, don't sign, and complain as for a company worker (see ["If an agency, broker or relative takes your money"](#if-an-agency-broker-or-relative-takes-your-money)) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How can a worker in Bangladesh see her Musaned contract herself before departure?

<a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women"></a>

### 3.3 Step 3: Do the one-month housekeeping course (women)

The course is compulsory for women going as domestic workers and runs at BMET's Technical Training Centres (TTCs) <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>. Brokers sell course certificates; don't buy one <sup>[N155 (2018)](#n155)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-1"></a>**Gather your papers;** bring the originals to the interview <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>:
   - your class 5 certificate (if any)
   - your guardian's no-objection letter
   - a photocopy of your birth certificate, NID or passport
2. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-2"></a>**Apply on OEP** ([employee.oep.gov.bd](https://employee.oep.gov.bd/employee/login-with-otp)) <sup>[40](#o40)</sup>:
   1. Log in with your mobile number and code.
   2. Open Training, then Trade/Skills.
   3. Choose division, district and centre, and press Search.
   4. Pick the housekeeping course, press Apply, then Process to Enroll and Confirm. "My Course" shows your applications.

   You pay the fee and choose the centre and start date online, from BMET's admission calendar <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the housekeeping course fee?
   > - What is the course's exact name in the OEP list?

3. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-3"></a>**Interview** in person at the TTC: a committee checks your original papers and that you can read and write <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-4"></a>**Live at the centre for one month** (24 working days), attending 90% of sessions; it covers cooking, appliances, language, culture, personal safety, reaching the police and embassy, and first aid <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-5"></a>**Pass the final test:** 100 marks, pass mark 70%, 30 for language and 30 for practical work <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women-item-6"></a>**Download the certificate** online; it is valid 2 years. If you haven't gone abroad within 2 to 3 years, do a 3-day refresher; after 3 years, the full course. A returnee with a year abroad does the refresher <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-medical-check-visa-and-bmet-clearance"></a>

### 3.4 Step 4: Medical check, visa and BMET clearance

1. <a id="step-4-medical-check-visa-and-bmet-clearance-item-1"></a>**Medical check, Tasheer fingerprints and checking the visa:** as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 4: Medical check, visa and BMET clearance](#step-4-medical-check-visa-and-bmet-clearance) (the Wafid medical check), [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre) and [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself).
2. <a id="step-4-medical-check-visa-and-bmet-clearance-item-2"></a>**BMET clearance** as for a company worker (see [Get BMET clearance](#get-bmet-clearance)), also bringing proof of age and the guardian's letter <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-medical-check-visa-and-bmet-clearance-item-3"></a>**Before you fly,** save the embassy labour wing's free line 8001000125 <sup>[N156](#n156), [78](#o78)</sup> and the Probashi call centre 16135 (+880 8000102030 from abroad) <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>, in your phone and on paper.

<a id="scams-1"></a>

### 3.5 Scams

Age changes (see [Step 1: Check you qualify](#step-1-check-you-qualify)), bought certificates (see [Step 3: Do the one-month housekeeping course (women)](#step-3-do-the-one-month-housekeeping-course-women)) and fees taken from women (see [Step 2: Find the job through an approved agency](#step-2-find-the-job-through-an-approved-agency)) are covered at their steps <sup>[N154 (2020)](#n154), [N155 (2018)](#n155), [2](#o2)</sup>. Brokers also sell house-driver and domestic-worker visas from households that need no one; the worker arrives to no job, and working elsewhere is illegal <sup>[N128 (2024)](#n128), [113](#o113)</sup>.

<a id="your-rights-at-work-1"></a>

### 3.6 Your rights at work

- **Who arranges what:** the employer pays your medical test, iqama and renewal, and health insurance or treatment <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>. The Saudi recruitment office must tell you the work and pay, and house and support you if you leave or refuse the employer <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>.
- **Contract on Musaned:** you get an SMS link, enter your residence number and the code sent to you, and accept within 5 days; it is free (Musaned 920002866) <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>.
- **Insurance:** a contract made through a recruitment office on Musaned is insured for its first two years <sup>[119 (2023)](#o119)</sup>. It covers:
  - permanent disability from an accident <sup>[119 (2023)](#o119)</sup>
  - your unpaid wages if the employer dies or is disabled <sup>[119 (2023)](#o119)</sup>
  - sending your body and belongings home if you die <sup>[119 (2023)](#o119)</sup>
- **Probation:** probation lasts up to 90 days; either side may end the contract <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.
- **Hours:** you work at most 10 hours a day, with a half-hour break after every 5 hours, at least 8 hours' continuous rest, and one paid day off a week <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.
- **Pay:** you are paid the contract wage monthly, into a bank account or digital wallet in your name linked to Musaned through your mobile and iqama numbers <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6), [114](#o114)</sup>. At most a quarter can be deducted, only for damage you caused, an advance or an official order <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>. The Saudi-Bangladesh recruitment agreement set SR 800 a month <sup>[120 (2015)](#o120)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Is SR 800 a month still the agreed wage for a Bangladeshi domestic worker?

- **Housing, food and contact:** the employer must give you suitable housing and food or a food allowance <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>; the employer must let you contact your family and the embassy, and may not keep your passport, papers or belongings <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.
- **Leave:** you get up to 30 days' sick leave a year with a medical report, 15 on full pay, the rest on half <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>; after two years, if you renew, 30 days' paid leave and a return ticket <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.
- **Only your own job:** the employer may not make you work for others or in another job <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>. If you work for anyone else yourself: up to SR 2,000 fine, a work ban or both <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>; working on your own account, SR 10,000 and deportation <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>.
- **At the end:** you get your wages and dues within a week, or two if you end the contract, and end-of-service pay of one month's wage per four years <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>. Ending the contract without a valid reason costs two months' wages to the other side unless the contract says otherwise <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>; if you leave without one, you pay your ticket home <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.

<a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1"></a>

### 3.7 If something goes wrong

**You can leave and keep your rights** if the employer breaks the contract, lied about the work, assaults you or behaves immorally towards you, gives you dangerous work or rents you out <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.

**The labour ministry can move you to a new employer without your employer's consent** if <sup>[25 (2024)](#o25)</sup>:

- wages are unpaid for three months (in a row or not)
- nobody collects you from the recruitment office or shelter within 15 days
- no iqama is issued or renewed within 30 days
- you are rented out, given dangerous work or ill-treated
- the employer misses two dispute sessions
- the employer ends the contract during probation

To move yourself: see [Domestic worker to another household](#domestic-worker-to-another-household); see [Domestic worker to a company job](#domestic-worker-to-a-company-job).

**Complain in this order:**

1. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-1"></a>**The embassy labour wing.** Call 8001000125 free in Saudi Arabia <sup>[N156](#n156), [78](#o78)</sup>, or use the form at [bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/) <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>. *You'll need* <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>:
   - name, mobile and city
   - iqama or border number, and passport number
   - the problem type ("domestic worker problems" or "food, housing and pay problems")
   - a detailed description in Bangla or English
   - photos of your iqama and passport, if you have them

   For example: "I am a housemaid in [city]. My employer [name, phone] hasn't paid me for [N] months and keeps my passport. Please help me get my wages and go home." <sup>[59](#o59)</sup> Follow up on the "Your answer (SRS)" page ([bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/index.php/main/viewrecord/](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/index.php/main/viewrecord/)) by reference, iqama or mobile number <sup>[121](#o121), [62](#o62)</sup>. Jeddah consulate labour wing: +966 12 689 6276 ext 131 <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-2"></a>**If you must leave the house, go to the embassy or consulate,** not to a stranger: 8039 Dareen Street, Diplomatic Quarter, Riyadh <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. The labour wings run safe homes for women in Riyadh, Jeddah and Madinah, paid for by the Wage Earners' Welfare Board <sup>[123 (2024)](#o123), [80](#o80)</sup>; the wait to fly home has run from over a month to 6 months <sup>[N157 (2020)](#n157)</sup>. Leaving without telling anyone can bring an absence (huroob) report, which makes you illegal (see ["If your employer reports you absent (huroob)"](#if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-huroob)) <sup>[N158 (2024)](#n158), [25 (2024)](#o25)</sup>; the employer can withdraw it within 15 days of filing, after which it is final <sup>[25 (2024)](#o25)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-3"></a>**The Saudi labour ministry.** File online through its "File a domestic labour lawsuit" service; the company-worker settlement service doesn't take domestic cases <sup>[124 (2024)](#o124), [96](#o96)</sup>. File within 12 months of the contract ending; the ministry tries to settle within 5 working days, then a committee or the labour court decides <sup>[113](#o113), [6 (2024)](#o6), [124 (2024)](#o124)</sup>. Musaned: 920002866 <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>. The labour wing helps settle disputes and attends court with workers <sup>[62](#o62)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the link to the "File a domestic labour lawsuit" service?
   > - What are the steps to file through it?
   > - Does it work without Absher?

4. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-4"></a>**From Bangladesh, a relative can act,** if the worker can't use a phone freely <sup>[N158 (2024)](#n158)</sup>; the labour wing can then trace the employer and recover wages <sup>[N159 (2022)](#n159)</sup>. The routes:
   - **A woman worker's case:** apply to the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare, Welfare-2 branch (aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd), describing what happened, with copies of her passport, BMET smart card and iqama, the agency's details and the accused person's mobile and email; it's free, and within 7 working days the ministry writes to the labour wing or BMET <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
   - **To bring her home:** apply the same way with proof she is a migrant worker, her workplace and home address in Saudi Arabia, the agency's name and address and the employer's mobile <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
   - **Unpaid wages of any worker, man or woman:** use the Welfare-1 branch route (see [If you aren't paid, item 4: From Bangladesh, a relative can ask the government to act](#if-you-arent-paid-item-4)) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
   - **If nothing happens:** write to the Joint Secretary (Mission and Welfare), 02-41030219, adsmission@probashi.gov.bd <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
   - **A safe-home place:** a relative can request one, free, through the probashi kallyan (expatriates' welfare) branch at the Deputy Commissioner's office, the DEMO or the Welfare Board <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.
   - **Advice:** Probashi call centre 16135 <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.
5. <a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1-item-5"></a>**Legal help.** OKUP gives free legal advice and help filing cases: 01842773300 <sup>[N142](#n142)</sup>.

<a id="if-you-are-sexually-abused"></a>

#### 3.7.1 If you are sexually abused

The labour ministry's abuse-reporting line, 1919, takes reports of physical, psychological or sexual abuse round the clock, in confidence <sup>[125](#o125)</sup>. Sex outside marriage is a crime there, and hospitals can report a pregnancy outside marriage to the police, even one from rape <sup>[N160 (2024)](#n160)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the 1919 line answer in Bangla or English?
> - How does the labour wing get a pregnant worker medical care and home?
> - How does a baby born there to a domestic worker get travel papers?

<a id="house-drivers"></a>

### 3.8 House drivers

- **Cost and pay.** Agencies have asked Tk 4-6 lakh for a house-driver visa <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>, though the employer pays recruitment (see [Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker)) <sup>[113](#o113), [6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>. Get any payment agreed in writing and pay only into the agency's bank account (see [Before you pay anyone](#before-you-pay-anyone)). New private drivers earn about SR 1,500-1,800 a month <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>.
- **Check the visa.** Brokers promise a company job and send the worker as a private driver, or never arrange the licence <sup>[N44](#n44)</sup>. Check the visa (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 7: Check the visa yourself](#step-7-check-the-visa-yourself)); it shows the occupation and the sponsor's name <sup>[53](#o53), [N49 (2024)](#n49)</sup>. The occupation must be private driver and the sponsor the household you were told about <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6), [N49 (2024)](#n49)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - How is "private driver" written in Arabic on the visa?

- **Licence.** The employer arranges and pays for your Saudi licence <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>. With a Bangladeshi licence translated by an accredited agency, you can drive on it for at most 3 months <sup>[N161 (2023)](#n161)</sup>. For the Saudi licence <sup>[N162](#n162)</sup>:
  1. Book a driving school on Absher (Traffic Services, Book Driving School).
  2. Do the medical at an approved clinic.
  3. Open a file at the school; a beginner course costs about SR 2,500-3,000.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Is the official Saudi driving theory test offered in Bangla?

- **Crashes.** A fatal crash can mean blood money and prison (see [Driving](#driving)).

<a id="umrah-visa"></a>

## 4 Umrah visa

The Umrah visa is for Muslims, all year except the Hajj season (15 Dhul Qa'dah to 15 Dhul Hijjah) <sup>[126](#o126)</sup>. It costs USD 80 plus USD 10.50 visa service fee, USD 10.50 insurance service fee and the insurance; it is single entry, valid 90 days, with a stay of up to 90 days <sup>[126](#o126)</sup>. Packages cost about Tk 1.2 lakh to Tk 3 lakh or more <sup>[N163](#n163)</sup>.

1. <a id="umrah-visa-item-1"></a>**Choose a licensed agency.** Bangladeshis can't apply on Nusuk, the Saudi Umrah platform, themselves <sup>[N164](#n164), [126](#o126)</sup>. Pay only an agency on the Ministry of Religious Affairs' yearly list of licensed Hajj and Umrah agencies, on its website and [hajj.gov.bd](https://hajj.gov.bd/) <sup>[N165](#n165)</sup>. Fake agencies advertise on Facebook and YouTube <sup>[N165](#n165)</sup>.
2. <a id="umrah-visa-item-2"></a>**Give the agency your papers;** the agent books a Nusuk-approved hotel and transport <sup>[N164](#n164)</sup>. *You'll need* <sup>[N164](#n164)</sup>:
   - a passport valid 6 months with 2 blank pages
   - a white-background photo
   - a meningitis (ACWY) certificate from at least 10 days before arrival
