3. <a id="umrah-visa-item-3"></a>**Enrol face and fingerprints** after applying, in Saudi Visa Bio, the Foreign Ministry's free app on Google Play and the App Store, with your email and a photo of your passport; anyone 6 or older, under-18s with an adult <sup>[127](#o127), [N164](#n164)</sup>.
4. <a id="umrah-visa-item-4"></a>**Travel within 30 days** of issue, or the visa is cancelled <sup>[N166](#n166)</sup>.

The visa doesn't allow work or staying on for Hajj <sup>[126](#o126), [N167 (2024)](#n167)</sup>: illegal work brings a SR 10,000 fine and deportation the first time <sup>[6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>.

**If the agency cheats you,** write to the Secretary, Ministry of Religious Affairs, with what you paid, when and how, what wasn't delivered, and the agency's name, licence number and address; the ministry can order a refund within 7 working days or act against the agency <sup>[128](#o128)</sup>. Hajj and Umrah section: +880 2 55100899, morahajsection@gmail.com <sup>[128](#o128)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a refused Umrah visa be appealed, and how?
> - Who helps a pilgrim stranded in Saudi Arabia when the agency fails?

<a id="hajj-visa"></a>

## 5 Hajj visa

Hajj visas are issued only within Bangladesh's yearly quota, through the Ministry of Religious Affairs; the 2027 quota of 78,500 is full and registration has closed <sup>[N168](#n168)</sup>. Children under 15, people with serious chronic or infectious illness, and women late in or at high risk in pregnancy can't go <sup>[N169](#n169)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - When does pre-registration for the next Hajj open?
> - How is the opening of pre-registration announced?

1. <a id="hajj-visa-item-1"></a>**Pre-register:** Tk 30,000, with NID or passport (birth registration under 18) <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>.
   - Government package: on [hajj.gov.bd](https://hajj.gov.bd/) or at a DC office, Islamic Foundation office, Union Digital Centre or the Dhaka Hajj Office <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>.
   - Private package: through a licensed agency on the ministry's list <sup>[N165](#n165)</sup>.
2. <a id="hajj-visa-item-2"></a>**Primary registration:** deposit Tk 3.5 lakh <sup>[N169](#n169)</sup>.
   - Government package: at any Sonali Bank branch with a voucher <sup>[N169](#n169)</sup>.
   - Private package: into the agency's account shown on the Hajj portal <sup>[N169](#n169)</sup>.
3. <a id="hajj-visa-item-3"></a>**Final registration:** pay the rest by the deadline (10 December 2026 for Hajj 2027) <sup>[N169](#n169)</sup>. 2027 government packages cost Tk 5,05,648 to Tk 6,15,263, the private standard package Tk 5,13,648 <sup>[N170](#n170)</sup>.
4. <a id="hajj-visa-item-4"></a>**Give the agency your passport and registration certificate, and do biometrics** in Saudi Visa Bio <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>.
5. <a id="hajj-visa-item-5"></a>**Collect passport, ticket, visa, ID card and luggage tags** before the flight <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>.

Hajj on any other visa brings up to SR 20,000 fine, deportation and a ban of up to 10 years <sup>[N171](#n171)</sup>.

**Questions and complaints:** helpline 16136 (+880 9602666707 from abroad in office hours) <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>; against a private agency that took money and didn't deliver, complain as for an Umrah agency (see [Umrah visa](#umrah-visa), If the agency cheats you) <sup>[128](#o128)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the Hajj office's complaint route for services missing during Hajj?

<a id="family-residence"></a>

## 6 Family residence

Family residence is for a worker's spouse, sons under 25 and unmarried daughters, if the worker's permit is graded skilled <sup>[93](#o93)</sup>. Skilled means occupational groups 4 to 8 with pay above a ministry threshold and professional accreditation; elementary (group 9) jobs or lower pay are basic; the grade shows in your employer's Qiwa account and can be corrected ([Bringing your family](#bringing-your-family)) <sup>[58](#o58)</sup>. The family can get entry visas with the worker's work visa, finishing residence after arrival <sup>[93](#o93)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Did the skilled-grade rule replace the SR 3,500 salary condition (see [Family residence, item 1: Check you qualify](#family-residence-item-1))?
> - Do basic-level workers get family visas at all?

1. <a id="family-residence-item-1"></a>**Check you qualify:** a salary of at least SR 3,500 a month on your GOSI (General Organization for Social Insurance) record <sup>[N172](#n172)</sup>.
2. <a id="family-residence-item-2"></a>**Prepare the papers in Bangladesh,** attested and translated into Arabic <sup>[N172](#n172)</sup>:
   - the marriage certificate
   - children's birth certificates
   - your degree certificate

   An apostille replaces embassy legalisation between the two countries <sup>[130](#o130), [131](#o131)</sup>; get one on [mygov.bd](https://www.mygov.bd/) as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 6: Give the agency your papers for the visa](#step-6-give-the-agency-your-papers-for-the-visa) <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Do Absher and the Saudi embassy accept an apostille instead of attestation for these papers?

3. <a id="family-residence-item-3"></a>**Apply on Absher:** My Services, Expatriates Services, New Family Visa Application, or at the recruitment (Istiqdam) office if Absher shows an error <sup>[N172](#n172)</sup>. Print and sign the form; your company's HR attests it on the Chamber of Commerce site within 30 days (SR 30) <sup>[N172](#n172)</sup>.
4. <a id="family-residence-item-4"></a>**Pay the SR 2,000 visa fee** through SADAD, the Saudi bill-payment system in your bank's app, with your iqama and application numbers <sup>[N172](#n172)</sup>; after 2-3 working days Absher shows the visa number; send it to your family <sup>[N172](#n172)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is the SR 2,000 visa fee charged once per family or for each family member?

5. <a id="family-residence-item-5"></a>**The family applies through Tasheer** in Dhaka or Chattogram, which checks papers, takes biometrics and fees and sends the passports to the embassy <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>. Book as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre) <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>, bring a medical report <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>, and track with Track Status on vc.tasheer.com <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What documents does Tasheer require for a family residence visa?
   > - What is Tasheer's fee for it?
   > - How long does Tasheer take to process it?
   > - Can a refused family residence visa be appealed?

After arrival, each dependant costs SR 400 a month after the first 90 days <sup>[N172](#n172)</sup>. A dependant can work in health or education through Ajeer (see [Family dependant to work](#family-dependant-to-work)) <sup>[N173 (2024)](#n173)</sup>. A child of a Bangladeshi parent is Bangladeshi <sup>[132](#o132)</sup>.

<a id="if-a-baby-is-born-in-saudi-arabia"></a>

### 6.1 If a baby is born in Saudi Arabia

Register the baby on Absher (My Services, Services, Civil Affairs, Newborn Registration) within 30 days, or pay a fine <sup>[N174](#n174)</sup>. For the baby's passport:

1. <a id="if-a-baby-is-born-in-saudi-arabia-item-1"></a>**Get the Saudi birth certificate** (Shahada Milad) <sup>[133](#o133)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-a-baby-is-born-in-saudi-arabia-item-2"></a>**Get a Bangladeshi birth registration** at the Jeddah consulate or at a parent's permanent address, with Saudi Arabia as place of birth <sup>[133](#o133)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-a-baby-is-born-in-saudi-arabia-item-3"></a>**Apply for the passport** at [passport.gov.bd](http://www.passport.gov.bd/) with <sup>[133](#o133)</sup>:
   - the Saudi birth certificate
   - copies of both parents' passports and iqamas
   - the Bangladeshi birth certificate
   - an A4 colour photo of the baby on grey or white
   - passport photos of the baby and a parent

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the fee for the baby's Bangladeshi passport?
   > - How long does the passport take?
   > - Does the Riyadh embassy follow the same steps?

<a id="family-visit-visa"></a>

## 7 Family visit visa

This visa is for relatives of a worker in Saudi Arabia; it is valid 90 days, allows a stay of up to 90 days and costs USD 80 plus USD 21 service fees and insurance <sup>[134](#o134)</sup>. Bangladeshis without a US, UK or Schengen visit visa apply through a visa service centre <sup>[134](#o134)</sup>. Umrah is allowed, Hajj is not <sup>[134](#o134)</sup>. Around the Hajj season Saudi Arabia can suspend these visas and order holders out, on pain of fines and a ban <sup>[N175](#n175)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Will Saudi Arabia suspend family visit visas again before Hajj 2027?

1. <a id="family-visit-visa-item-1"></a>**Your relative applies** on [visa.mofa.gov.sa](https://visa.mofa.gov.sa/); the relative's work iqama needs at least 3-4 months left <sup>[N176](#n176)</sup>. The steps <sup>[14](#o14)</sup>:
   1. Log in with Nafath, the Saudi digital ID app.
   2. Choose Family visit visa and press Apply.
   3. Fill in the form, press Add and enter your details exactly as in your passport.
   4. Approve the agreement, Save and print.
2. <a id="family-visit-visa-item-2"></a>**The employer approves it** online ("Ratify individuals applications"), a domestic worker's employer too <sup>[14](#o14), [N176](#n176)</sup>; your relative sends you the visa document number <sup>[N176](#n176)</sup>.
3. <a id="family-visit-visa-item-3"></a>**You apply through Tasheer** (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre)) with that number and proof of the relationship, such as a marriage or birth certificate, and track it with Track Status on vc.tasheer.com <sup>[N176](#n176), [N28](#n28)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What documents does Tasheer require for a family visit visa?
   > - What is Tasheer's fee for it?
   > - How long does Tasheer take to process it?
   > - Can a refused family visit visa be appealed?

The visa doesn't allow work: a visit visa can't become a job ([Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type)) <sup>[N167 (2024)](#n167), [6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>.

<a id="tourist-visa"></a>

## 8 Tourist visa

Bangladeshis can't get the online e-visa without a US, UK or Schengen visit visa <sup>[134](#o134), [135](#o135)</sup>.

**Through Tasheer:** the visa costs USD 80 plus USD 21 service fees and insurance, and lasts 90 days <sup>[134](#o134), [135](#o135)</sup>.

**Package visa:** this is an instant e-visa sold with confirmed flights and hotel by a travel agent approved by Saudi Arabia's Ministry of Tourism, open to Bangladeshis <sup>[136](#o136), [137](#o137)</sup>. It must include a round-trip flight and a licensed 4-star-plus hotel, at least SR 4,000 per adult for two days plus SR 1,000 a day after <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>. Buy only from a partner listed on [Visit Saudi](https://www.visitsaudi.com/en/campaigns/package-visa) <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>:

- Reserval ([reserval.com/packagevisa](https://www.reserval.com/packagevisa))
- Almosafer
- webook.com ([webook.com/en/e-visa](https://webook.com/en/e-visa))
- Almatar ([almatar.com](https://almatar.com/en/?visaType=tourist-visa))

1. Choose the package <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.
2. Upload passport details and a photo <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.
3. Pay; an automatic check follows <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.

The visa is issued within 48 hours, valid 3 months, single entry, unchangeable after issue <sup>[136](#o136)</sup>.

**If refused:**

> **Need to know**
> - Can a refused tourist visa be appealed?
> - Who takes a complaint about a package-visa seller?

<a id="can-you-work"></a>

### 8.1 Can you work?

A tourist visa is not a work visa: brokers sell 3-month tourist visas as jobs with a "15-month iqama", and the worker becomes illegal <sup>[N128 (2024)](#n128), [6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>.

<a id="business-visit-visa"></a>

## 9 Business visit visa

The business visit visa is for meetings, conferences and partnerships, not a permanent work contract; a "business visa" for a factory job is illegal work <sup>[138](#o138), [6 (2024)](#o6)</sup>. It costs USD 80 plus USD 10.50 visa service fee, USD 10.50 insurance service fee and the insurance; single entry lasts 90 days and multiple entry a year, with stays up to 90 days <sup>[138](#o138)</sup>.

1. <a id="business-visit-visa-item-1"></a>**Get the invitation letter** from the Saudi business <sup>[138](#o138)</sup>.
2. <a id="business-visit-visa-item-2"></a>**Apply through Tasheer** <sup>[N28](#n28)</sup>.
3. <a id="business-visit-visa-item-3"></a>**Get the visa** 1-2 working days after the letter and the application <sup>[138](#o138)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What documents does a business visit visa application need?
> - How is the invitation letter attested?
> - Can a refused business visit visa be appealed?

<a id="student-visa"></a>

## 10 Student visa

Applying on [studyinsaudi.sa](https://studyinsaudi.sa/), the Ministry of Education's platform, is free; agents who charge to apply are not authorised <sup>[N177](#n177)</sup>. Official Saudi sites end in .sa or gov.sa <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>. Full scholarships have covered tuition, airfare, housing, meals, books and a stipend <sup>[140](#o140), [N178](#n178)</sup>.

1. <a id="student-visa-item-1"></a>**Check you qualify** <sup>[N177](#n177)</sup>:
   - ages 17 to 25 for a bachelor's, up to 30 for a master's, 35 for a PhD
   - IELTS 5.5 or English-medium schooling for courses in English

   > **Need to know**
   > - When does the next application window open, given the last one closed on 6 October 2026 <sup>[N177](#n177)</sup>?

2. <a id="student-visa-item-2"></a>**Apply** to up to 5 universities, 3 programmes each <sup>[N177](#n177)</sup>, uploading <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>:
   - a valid passport
   - a medical report showing no infectious disease
   - attested certificates
   - whatever the university asks

   Only contact details can be changed after submitting <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.
3. <a id="student-visa-item-3"></a>**Wait for the decision,** which comes only through the platform: the university approves, then it and the ministry complete admission; a rejection means a requirement wasn't met <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>. Help: a support ticket on the platform <sup>[139](#o139)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How can a student confirm an admission letter directly with the university?

4. <a id="student-visa-item-4"></a>**Get the study visa,** renewed yearly <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>. It needs <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>:
   - admission from an accredited institution
   - proof of funds or a scholarship
   - a medical report
   - medical insurance
   - proof of the language of study

   > **Need to know**
   > - Where does a Bangladeshi student get the study visa issued: Tasheer or the embassy?
   > - What is the study visa fee?
   > - How long does processing take?
   > - Can a refused study visa be appealed?

<a id="other-visas"></a>

## 11 Other visas

<a id="temporary-work-visa"></a>

### 11.1 Temporary work visa

A Saudi employer requests it on Qiwa (the labour ministry's work platform) for project or contract work; it lasts 90 days plus one 90-day extension, gives no iqama, and the employer pays its SR 1,000 fee <sup>[N179](#n179)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Who finds the job for a Bangladeshi on a temporary work visa?
> - Are a recruiting agency and BMET clearance involved?
> - What does the worker pay?
> - What scams run on this route?

<a id="hajj-seasonal-work-visa"></a>

### 11.2 Hajj seasonal work visa

This visa is for Hajj-season work only: entry is from 1 Shawwal for a 4-month stay, it can't be renewed or converted to a work visa, and the entry fee is SR 1,000 <sup>[N180 (2023)](#n180)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do Bangladeshis get the Hajj seasonal work visa?
> - Through whom do they get it?

<a id="stopover-transit-visa"></a>

### 11.3 Stopover (transit) visa

This visa is for Saudia passengers flying through Saudi Arabia, open to Bangladeshis <sup>[N181](#n181), [N182 (2024)](#n182)</sup>. *You'll need* <sup>[N181](#n181)</sup>:

- a passport valid 6 months
- a confirmed onward ticket
- a visa for the final destination
- a 200 x 200 pixel photo of at most 20 KB

1. <a id="stopover-transit-visa-item-1"></a>**Apply on saudia.com while booking** (Seats & Extras or Additional Services) <sup>[N181](#n181)</sup>.
2. <a id="stopover-transit-visa-item-2"></a>**Pay with the flight** <sup>[N181](#n181)</sup>.
3. <a id="stopover-transit-visa-item-3"></a>**The visa and insurance come by email** <sup>[N181](#n181)</sup>.

It allows one stay of up to 96 hours, with no extension; passport control decides entry <sup>[N181](#n181)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the stopover visa fee?
> - Is any work allowed on a stopover visa?

<a id="premium-residency"></a>

### 11.4 Premium Residency

Premium Residency is residence not tied to an employer, sold by the Premium Residency Center at pr.gov.sa <sup>[141](#o141)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does Premium Residency cost?
> - Who qualifies for Premium Residency?

<a id="medical-treatment-visa"></a>

### 11.5 Medical treatment visa

Bangladeshis without a US, UK or Schengen visa, or US, UK, EU or Gulf residence, apply in two steps <sup>[142](#o142)</sup>:

1. <a id="medical-treatment-visa-item-1"></a>**Apply on ksavisa.sa** <sup>[142](#o142)</sup>.
2. <a id="medical-treatment-visa-item-2"></a>**Then go through a visa service centre or the Saudi embassy** <sup>[142](#o142)</sup>. In Dhaka the visa centre is run by Tasheer (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 5: Give fingerprints at the Tasheer visa centre](#step-5-give-fingerprints-at-the-tasheer-visa-centre)) <sup>[N115 (2023)](#n115), [N114 (2023)](#n114)</sup>.

It costs USD 80 plus two USD 10.50 digital-service fees and the insurance, priced by the insurer you choose; a single-entry visa lasts 90 days and a multiple-entry one a year, with stays up to 90 days; processing takes a minute to 3 working days; it doesn't allow Hajj <sup>[142](#o142)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What documents does the medical treatment visa need?
> - Does Tasheer's Dhaka centre take medical visa applications?

<a id="changing-visa-type"></a>

## 12 Changing visa type

**Company worker to another company:** see [If you want to change employer](#if-you-want-to-change-employer).

<a id="visit-tourist-umrah-or-business-visa-to-work"></a>

### 12.1 Visit, tourist, Umrah or business visa to work

A visit visa can't become an iqama, family visitors may not work, and an Umrah visa may not be used for work <sup>[N66 (2022)](#n66), [N167 (2024)](#n167)</sup>; anyone charging to "convert" one is lying <sup>[N66 (2022)](#n66)</sup>. Working on a tourist or Umrah visa can bring jail, deportation and a 3 to 10 year ban ([Going home without valid papers](#going-home-without-valid-papers)).

**A seasonal work visa can't be converted to an iqama** <sup>[N180 (2023)](#n180)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a temporary work visa be converted to an iqama?

<a id="family-dependant-to-work"></a>

### 12.2 Family dependant to work

A spouse or mahram (a close male guardian relative) on a dependant iqama can work for a health or education employer, staying on the family sponsorship, through Ajeer ([ajeer.qiwa.sa](https://ajeer.qiwa.sa)) <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27), [143 (2024)](#o143)</sup>.

1. <a id="family-dependant-to-work-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27), [N173 (2024)](#n173)</sup>:
   - valid iqamas for you and the main worker;
   - age 18 or over;
   - any licence the job requires;
   - an offer from a health or education employer rated low green or higher on Nitaqat, the Saudisation rating.
2. <a id="family-dependant-to-work-item-2"></a>**The employer issues the contract on Ajeer** (Contracts, Issue Contract, Contracting Service Between Workforce, Ajeer Accompanying Service); once approved, Ajeer issues your work permit, free <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27), [N173 (2024)](#n173)</sup>. If it stalls, call Ajeer on 920011040 or the labour ministry on 19911 <sup>[143 (2024)](#o143), [100 (2024)](#o100)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How long does Ajeer take to issue the work permit after the employer issues the contract?

> **Need to know**
> - Can a dependant on a family iqama work for an employer outside health and education?
> - If so, through which platform or procedure does the dependant get the work permit?

<a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household"></a>

### 12.3 Domestic worker to another household

The transfer is done on Musaned ([musaned.gov.sa](https://musaned.gov.sa)) <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27), [113](#o113)</sup>.

1. <a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[N183 (2023)](#n183), [27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>:
   - the agreement of your current employer, you and the new employer;
   - your own Absher account.
2. <a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household-item-2"></a>**Your employer starts the transfer on Musaned** <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>.
3. <a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household-item-3"></a>**The new employer accepts or rejects it there** <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>.
4. <a id="domestic-worker-to-another-household-item-4"></a>**A new contract is drawn up and the request completed through Absher** <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>. You get a new fixed-period work contract <sup>[N183 (2023)](#n183)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How long does a Musaned transfer to another household take?
   > - How can the worker see the new contract's terms before agreeing to the transfer?

You pay nothing: the service is free and the employer bears transfer fees; payments between employers go through Musaned, capped at SR 16,188 for a Bangladeshi worker <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27), [113](#o113), [N183 (2023)](#n183)</sup>.

**If your employer refuses or there is a money dispute,** file a free claim with the domestic workers' dispute committees on the ministry's Individuals Portal or unified app (My Services, Settlement Claims for Domestic Workers) with your original iqama or passport <sup>[27 (2024)](#o27)</sup>. Musaned: 920002866 <sup>[118](#o118)</sup>; embassy labour wing, free: 8001000125 <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a domestic worker move to another household without the current employer's consent?

<a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job"></a>

### 12.4 Domestic worker to a company job

You can move to a company job only in your first year; after your iqama is renewed the request is refused <sup>[N184 (2022)](#n184)</sup>.

1. <a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[N184 (2022)](#n184)</sup>:
   - a company that has signed a work contract with you;
   - your mobile number, to which Qiwa sends the request.
2. <a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job-item-2"></a>**The company requests the transfer on Qiwa,** naming your new job <sup>[N184 (2022)](#n184)</sup>.
3. <a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job-item-3"></a>**Qiwa sends you an SMS;** approve or reject it within 10 days <sup>[N184 (2022)](#n184)</sup>.
4. <a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job-item-4"></a>**Your household employer is then asked to agree** <sup>[N184 (2022)](#n184)</sup>. Qiwa support: 920000105 <sup>[144](#o144)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What happens to the transfer request if the household employer refuses?
   > - What does a transfer from a household to a company cost, and who pays it?
   > - How long does a transfer from a household to a company take?

<a id="changing-the-job-title-on-the-permit"></a>

### 12.5 Changing the job title on the permit

Only an employer in the platinum or green Nitaqat band can change it, on Qiwa (Change expatriate profession); it is free and takes effect at once, but a job reserved for Saudis can't be given to a foreign worker <sup>[144](#o144), [30](#o30)</sup>. Since January 2026, titles including sales representative, marketing specialist and procurement manager can't be given to foreign workers this way <sup>[N185](#n185)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What can a worker do if the employer refuses to change the job title on the iqama?

<a id="asylum"></a>

## 13 Asylum

Saudi Arabia has no asylum system, hasn't signed the 1951 Refugee Convention and generally doesn't grant asylum <sup>[N186](#n186)</sup>. People there illegally, including pilgrimage overstayers, are refused refugee status and deported <sup>[N186](#n186)</sup>. The only protection is UNHCR, the UN refugee agency: people it recognises may stay temporarily, with valid identity papers, until resettled or returned; those without travel documents are sometimes detained indefinitely <sup>[N186](#n186)</sup>.

Apply by email to saprstaf@unhcr.org with <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>:

- your full name;
- family members' names;
- date of birth, sex and nationality;
- address and phone;
- any earlier UNHCR registration;
- the reason.

Toll-free line 009668001000281, Sunday, Tuesday and Thursday <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>. The Riyadh office, in the Diplomatic Quarter, is by appointment only <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>.

<a id="sending-money-home"></a>

## 14 Sending money home

**Use a legal channel,** never hundi (the illegal informal channel) or unlicensed agents <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>. Saudi police have arrested people for sending money outside legal channels, and Bangladesh has frozen bKash, Nagad and Rocket accounts linked to hundi <sup>[N187 (2021)](#n187), [N188 (2023)](#n188)</sup>. Without a valid iqama you can't use a bank or send money formally <sup>[19](#o19)</sup>.

**Compare costs** on the World Bank's price comparison, [remittanceprices.worldbank.org/corridor/SA/BD](https://remittanceprices.worldbank.org/corridor/SA/BD). Sending SR 750 cost <sup>[146](#o146)</sup>:

- about 7-9% through STC Pay, TeleMoney, Western Union or MoneyGram; STC Pay delivers in under an hour to cash pickup, a bank account or a mobile wallet;
- about 15-16% at bank branches, which charge SR 75.

> **Need to know**
> - What documents do STC Pay, TeleMoney and the other remittance apps need to open an account?

**To a bKash account:**

1. <a id="sending-money-home-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[N189](#n189)</sup>:
   - the family member's bKash number;
   - their full name exactly as registered when the account was opened;
   - the cash or money in your app.
2. <a id="sending-money-home-item-2"></a>**Use a bKash partner:** Saudi Awwal Bank, STC Pay, URpay, Mobily Pay, tiqmo, barq, Western Union or MoneyGram <sup>[N189](#n189)</sup>.
3. <a id="sending-money-home-item-3"></a>**Give the number and name with the cash, or enter them in its app** <sup>[N189](#n189)</sup>.

The limit is Tk 2.5 lakh per transfer, as many times as you like <sup>[N189](#n189)</sup>. Your family gets the money plus the 2.5% government incentive automatically, and can cash out at BRAC Bank, City Bank and Q-Cash ATMs for Tk 7 per thousand <sup>[N190](#n190), [N191 (2022)](#n191), [N189](#n189)</sup>.

**If money doesn't arrive or goes to the wrong number:**

1. Complain to the sender company and bKash (16247) <sup>[N189](#n189), [147](#o147)</sup>.
2. Then complain to the company's complaint cell <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.
3. Then complain to Bangladesh Bank's Customer Interest Protection Centre (16236 during office hours on working days, bb.cipc@bb.org.bd, or its online form) with a description, your name, address and phone, and the provider's final reply; a relative needs your written authorisation <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.

<a id="going-home-without-valid-papers"></a>

## 15 Going home without valid papers

Overstaying any visa can bring a fine up to SR 50,000, up to 6 months in prison and deportation <sup>[148](#o148)</sup>. The re-entry ban, from departure, is usually 3 to 5 years for overstaying or huroob and 3 to 10 years for working on a tourist or Umrah visa <sup>[N59](#n59)</sup>. Leaving without a legitimate reason means paying your own ticket <sup>[30](#o30)</sup>.

**The Special Exit Programme (SEP)** is the Bangladesh Embassy's route to an exit visa.

1. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-1"></a>**Check you qualify:**
   - You qualify if your sponsor's company is under the labour office of Riyadh, Al Qassim, Hail, Al Jouf, Northern Borders or the Eastern Province, and your iqama has expired, was never issued or carries a huroob report; house drivers and male domestic workers with an expired iqama also qualify <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
   - Excluded: women domestic workers; anyone who didn't leave on an earlier exit visa; anyone with a police or court case or wanted (matlub), who must settle it with that authority first <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
2. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-2"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>:
   - your iqama number;
   - your original iqama;
   - a passport copy showing its validity.
3. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-3"></a>**Apply online** at [bangladeshembassy.org.sa/sepsrv.html](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/sepsrv.html) ("Special Exit programme(SEP) Application Form"); no visit or call needed <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
4. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-4"></a>**Check progress** with your iqama number at [bangladeshembassy.org.sa/seppub](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/seppub/); when an appointment appears, print it and go to the embassy on that day with the documents <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
5. <a id="going-home-without-valid-papers-item-5"></a>**Get the exit visa:**
   - For a Riyadh company, the embassy gets labour office clearance; you then hand in your original iqama and passport copy, and the embassy sends them to Jawazat for the exit visa <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.
   - For a company under another labour office, or a huroob case with a sponsor outside Riyadh, the embassy gives you a letter to take to that labour office or Jawazat, where you collect the exit visa <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

There is no time limit; workers have waited months, mostly without work <sup>[N192 (2023)](#n192)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does applying through SEP cost anything?
> - Must Saudi fines be paid before SEP issues an exit visa?
> - What can a worker do if an SEP application is refused?

**Sponsors under other labour offices** (Makkah, Madinah, Tabuk, Asir, Jazan, Najran, Al Baha) aren't covered by SEP <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>. Contact the Jeddah consulate's labour wing ([Contacts](#contacts)) <sup>[149](#o149)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the exit procedure for a worker without valid papers whose sponsor is under the Makkah, Madinah, Tabuk, Asir, Jazan, Najran or Al Baha labour office?

**Any other papers problem:**

1. Use the embassy's online form at [bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/srspub/), choosing the problem (huroob, no iqama, in jail, salary, domestic worker), describing it in Bangla or English and attaching iqama and passport copies <sup>[59](#o59)</sup>.
2. If there's no reply, call the labour wing free on 8001000125 <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.
3. Then have a relative write to the ministry (see [Help from Bangladesh](#help-from-bangladesh)) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does the embassy take to reply to the online request form?

**Without a passport** you need a travel permit (outpass) from the embassy's passport wing <sup>[N193](#n193), [78](#o78)</sup>. Either <sup>[78](#o78), [90](#o90)</sup>:

- call 8001000126 free; or
- book "Consular & Welfare Service" at [passportriyadh.com/appointment](https://passportriyadh.com/appointment/) with your name, phone, passport number and OID number.

In a deportation centre, embassy officials arrange it there <sup>[N193](#n193)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which documents does the passport wing need for a travel permit?
> - What is the travel permit's fee?
> - What is the OID number, and where does a worker find it?
> - How does a worker book the appointment without a passport number?

<a id="help-from-bangladesh"></a>

### 15.1 Help from Bangladesh

1. <a id="help-from-bangladesh-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>:
   - proof the person is a migrant worker (passport, BMET smart card, iqama or work permit copies);
   - the Saudi workplace and home addresses;
   - the agency's name and address;
   - the employer's mobile number.
2. <a id="help-from-bangladesh-item-2"></a>**Send an application** describing what happened, with these papers, to the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare, Welfare-2 branch (aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
3. <a id="help-from-bangladesh-item-3"></a>**It is free,** and within 7 working days the ministry asks the embassy, the Welfare Board or BMET to bring the worker home <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
4. <a id="help-from-bangladesh-item-4"></a>**If nothing happens,** write to the head of its Mission and Welfare wing (adsmission@probashi.gov.bd, 02-41030219, 01711427769) <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.

<a id="amnesties-and-grace-periods"></a>

### 15.2 Amnesties and grace periods

Saudi Arabia sometimes lets people with expired visas leave without a fine; the embassy posts these on [bangladeshembassy.org.sa/notice.html](https://www.bangladeshembassy.org.sa/notice.html) <sup>[N71](#n71), [150](#o150)</sup>. In a past window, people whose visit, Umrah or final exit visa expired on 25 February 2026 could leave without a fine until 18 April 2026 by going to the airport with a ticket <sup>[N71](#n71)</sup>. Until the end of 2026 an employer can regularise a work permit that expired more than 12 months ago or was never issued within 6 months of joining; cases not regularised then face legal measures <sup>[N72](#n72)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does a worker check whether their case qualifies for regularisation?
> - What can a worker do if the employer won't regularise the work permit?

<a id="if-you-are-detained"></a>

### 15.3 If you are detained

People picked up in raids have usually been held one to two weeks in a deportation centre, then flown straight home, often with nothing <sup>[N69 (2024)](#n69)</sup>. Wages owed can still be claimed after you leave: wage insurance if the whole company stopped paying (see [If you aren't paid, item 5: If the whole company has stopped paying, claim wage insurance](#if-you-arent-paid-item-5)), or a relative's application to the ministry's Welfare-1 branch (see [If you aren't paid, item 4: From Bangladesh, a relative can ask the government to act](#if-you-arent-paid-item-4)) <sup>[98](#o98), [69](#o69)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a friendly-settlement claim be filed, or continued, from Bangladesh after deportation?

<a id="if-a-worker-dies-is-injured-or-loses-the-job"></a>

## 16 If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job

**If a worker dies in Saudi Arabia:**

1. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-is-injured-or-loses-the-job-item-1"></a>**Call the embassy:** +966570212180, or the labour wing free on 8001000125 <sup>[N156](#n156), [78](#o78)</sup>. It registers the death, follows up with the employer and the authorities, and issues the no-objection certificate (NOC) needed for burial or repatriation <sup>[62](#o62)</sup>.

   The cause of death is recorded in Saudi Arabia, usually as "natural", and Bangladesh does not check it <sup>[N70](#n70)</sup>. The employer pays to prepare and fly the body home unless the family agrees to burial there <sup>[30](#o30)</sup>. If the worker had no valid papers, bringing the body home takes much longer <sup>[N70](#n70)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How can a family ask for a post-mortem in Saudi Arabia?
   > - How can a family challenge the recorded cause of death?

2. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-is-injured-or-loses-the-job-item-2"></a>**Tell the Wage Earners' Welfare Board** (Probashi Kallyan Bhaban, 71-72 Eskaton Garden, Dhaka) whether the family wants the body brought home or buried in Saudi Arabia; the Board relays this to the embassy and follows up until the body arrives, free <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>. It pays repatriation for workers who left with BMET clearance or joined it through the embassy, and asks the ministry to pay for others <sup>[N70](#n70)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How does the family apply to the Welfare Board to have the body brought home: in person, by email or on its portal?
   > - Which papers does the Board need from the family?
   > - How long does the Board take to get the body home?

   Or apply to the ministry's Welfare-2 branch (aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd) by email to bring the body home; free, action within 7 working days <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>. **You'll need** <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>:
   - a description;
   - the cause of death;
   - the death certificate;
   - copies of the worker's passport, work permit or iqama, BMET smart card and Saudi ID number.

   BRAC, a Bangladeshi development organisation, helps families apply through its Migration Programme ([Coming home](#coming-home)) <sup>[N194](#n194), [N70](#n70)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-a-worker-dies-is-injured-or-loses-the-job-item-3"></a>**At the airport,** the Board's desk calls the family, hands over the body at cargo with a Tk 35,000 cheque for transport and burial, and its ambulance carries the body from Dhaka, Chattogram or Sylhet airport, free <sup>[1](#o1), [80](#o80)</sup>. **You'll need** <sup>[80](#o80), [1](#o1)</sup>:
   - the worker's passport copy;
   - death certificate;
   - NOC;
   - original air waybill;
   - a heirship certificate from the Union Parishad (local council) chairman or ward councillor;
   - the wife's, father's or mother's NID;
   - any BMET clearance card;
   - a power of attorney if someone else collects.

**What the family can also claim:**

- **Tk 3 lakh Welfare Board grant,** for workers who left with BMET clearance or joined the Board through the embassy; the Board writes listing the papers <sup>[123 (2024)](#o123), [80](#o80)</sup>. **You'll need** <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>:
  - a family-members certificate from the Union Parishad chairman, mayor or councillor;
  - an undertaking on Tk 400 non-judicial stamp paper (samples on [wewb.gov.bd](https://www.wewb.gov.bd/));
  - a statement of the receiving account at any scheduled bank showing account and routing numbers;
  - 2 passport-size colour photos of each heir.

  Send them to the Board; it is free and pays into the account, with an SMS, within 60 working days of complete papers <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>, though families often have to chase it repeatedly <sup>[N70](#n70)</sup>.
- **Tk 10 lakh life insurance,** for workers who left with BMET clearance from 12 March 2023, covering 5 years from the BMET card's issue <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>. No application if the body comes home; if buried in Saudi Arabia or died after returning, apply at [portal.wewb.gov.bd](https://portal.wewb.gov.bd/) or the Board with the BMET card and passport copy; free, paid within 30 working days <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>. Claims recorded as suicide are often rejected. Middlemen offer to speed up payouts for a large cut; the claim is free and needs no middleman <sup>[N124](#n124)</sup>.
- **Compensation and unpaid wages from Saudi Arabia.** With the heirs' power of attorney, the embassy pursues them in Saudi courts and sends the money through BMET <sup>[62](#o62)</sup>. The Board tells the family when compensation is possible; the family prepares a power of attorney (the Board's legal cell helps) and the same four papers as for the grant; the Board pays heirs within 60 working days of receiving the money, free <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>. A relative can also email the ministry (aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd) with proof of migrant status, the employer's name and full address and what is owed; it instructs the embassy and Board within 7 working days, free <sup>[69](#o69)</sup>.
- **GOSI work-death compensation.** GOSI insures workers against work injury at the employer's cost; domestic workers only if its board decides so <sup>[88](#o88), [151](#o151)</sup>. For a work-injury death it pays the family 84 times the average monthly wage registered with it, plus repatriation <sup>[151](#o151)</sup>. Claim at a GOSI office (toll-free 199044), or from Bangladesh through the embassy with a power of attorney (see ["If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job"](#if-a-worker-dies-is-injured-or-loses-the-job), Compensation and unpaid wages from Saudi Arabia) <sup>[152](#o152), [62](#o62), [80](#o80)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Are domestic workers now covered by GOSI work-injury insurance?
  > - Which papers does GOSI need from the family for a work-death claim?
  > - How long does GOSI take to pay?

**Injured or seriously ill in Saudi Arabia:** apply to the Welfare Board with BMET clearance or membership and a passport copy; free, the Board gets the embassy to bring the worker home, sometimes into hospital, and the airport desk takes them to hospital by ambulance <sup>[80](#o80), [1](#o1)</sup>.

<a id="mental-health"></a>

### 16.1 Mental health

The Saudi Health Ministry's psychological consultation line, 920033360, takes calls from anyone in Saudi Arabia, 8 am to 8 pm (Saturday from 1 pm) <sup>[153](#o153)</sup>. After coming home, BRAC's Migration Programme gives counselling ([Coming home](#coming-home)) <sup>[N194](#n194)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does 920033360 answer in Bangla or English?
> - Does the embassy labour wing give counselling?
> - Does the Probashi call centre (16135) give counselling?

**Treatment money after coming home:** the Board pays up to Tk 2 lakh <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>. **You'll need** <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>:

- BMET clearance card or membership;
- passport copy;
- medical papers;
- a 6R colour photo.

Apply on the Board's portal, at a district welfare centre or at the Board; it checks by video call and pays within 60 working days <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.

**Disability insurance:** permanent disability within the 5 years pays 10% to 100% of Tk 10 lakh <sup>[37](#o37), [80](#o80)</sup>. **You'll need** <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>:

- BMET clearance card;
- passport copy;
- medical papers;
- a 6R photo showing the disability.

Apply free at the Board, paid within 30 working days (Deputy Director Welfare-2, +88 02 48317085, 01321-158354, dd.welfare2@wewb.gov.bd) <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.

**Job lost within 6 months and you come home:** the insurance pays Tk 50,000 <sup>[37](#o37), [80](#o80)</sup>. **You'll need** <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>:

- BMET clearance card;
- passport copy;
- copies of the departure and arrival stamp pages.

Apply free at the Board, paid within 30 working days (01712-107301, jahanwewb@gmail.com) <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>. Illness from before leaving isn't covered, and those returning for serious illness are often refused <sup>[N124](#n124)</sup>.

**If the Board doesn't deliver,** complain in this order <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>:

1. The officer named for the service.
2. The Board's grievance officer (+88 02 55138267, 01684-501891, azizwelfare@gmail.com, 30 working days).
3. The ministry's Joint Secretary (Administration) (+88 0241030261, jsadmin@probashi.gov.bd, 20 working days).
4. [grs.gov.bd](https://www.grs.gov.bd/), the government complaint system (60 working days).

<a id="coming-home"></a>

## 17 Coming home

<a id="what-you-can-bring-home"></a>

### 17.1 What you can bring home

- Duty-free yearly: 2 used phones and 1 new; 100 g gold and 200 g silver jewellery, at most 12 pieces of one kind; 200 cigarettes; and household items such as a TV up to 29 inches <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>.
- Gold: up to 117 g (10 tola), or one 10-tola bar, once a year if declared, at Tk 5,000 duty per tola; more is confiscated and undeclared bars mean arrest and a criminal case <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>.
- No alcohol on a Bangladeshi passport; goods to sell are seized <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>.
- Cash over USD 10,000 or equivalent: declare at customs on the FMJ form (the foreign-currency declaration form) <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>.

Declare goods on the form given on the plane, or at the custom house beside the airport within 7 days <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>. Customs: 13600 <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>.

**At Dhaka airport:**

- The Probashi Kallyan (expatriate welfare) desk helps with immigration, customs and the airline, free, and takes complaints from returnees abused or cheated abroad (+88 02 8901040, 01321-158352) <sup>[80](#o80), [N135](#n135)</sup>.
- Women returning within a year after abuse, torture or fraud get Tk 5,000 there. Bring <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>:
  - passport;
  - BMET clearance;
  - visa;
  - ticket;
  - air waybill.
- BRAC's Migration Programme helps returnees at the airport and with reintegration, counselling and jobs <sup>[N194](#n194)</sup>: Information Center, Rahman Villa (2nd floor), 402 Ashkona, Dakshinkhan, Dhaka, opposite the airport; 01700797182, 01700797183 <sup>[N195](#n195)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Does BRAC's Information Center serve workers returning from Saudi Arabia?

**A place to stay:**

- The Welfare Board's Wage Earners' Centre near Shahjalal airport: book at [wec.wewb.gov.bd](http://wec.wewb.gov.bd/wewb-centre/booking/search) or in person with BMET clearance or Board membership, passport copy and ticket; Tk 100 to register, Tk 200 a night, free airport transport (01321-158301, bwec@wewb.gov.bd) <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.
- The airport's Probashi Lounge gives workers with BMET clearance food at up to 30% off and free wifi, phone and printing <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.

**Legal help at home,** for example if your family is threatened: apply to your district probashi kallyan centre or the Board, which asks the local administration to act, free <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>. The centres are listed with contacts under "District Probashi Kallyan Centre list" on [wewb.gov.bd](https://wewb.gov.bd/) <sup>[155](#o155)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which papers must a returnee bring to the district centre or the Board for legal help inside Bangladesh?
> - How long does the local administration take to act?

**Probashi Kallyan Bank (PKB) reintegration loan** for starting a business: up to Tk 50 lakh over up to 10 years at 9% simple interest <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>. Security depends on the amount <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>:

- up to Tk 3 lakh: no collateral;
- Tk 3-5 lakh: a co-guarantee;
- above that: a registered land mortgage.

1. <a id="what-you-can-bring-home-item-1"></a>**You'll need** <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>:
   - the free application form;
   - 3 passport-size photos;
   - passport and NID copies;
   - a present and permanent address certificate from the Union Parishad or municipality;
   - a guarantor (relative or well-off person) with 2 photos, NID or passport copy, address certificate and a clean CIB report (the Credit Information Bureau report, Bangladesh Bank's record of a person's loans);
   - a current trade licence (compulsory);
   - a business description with address and 2 years' income and spending;
   - proof of rent or ownership of the premises;
   - a declaration of your own investment and other loans;
   - your papers from abroad;
   - any training certificate;
   - 3 signed cheque leaves each from the business's and the guarantor's accounts, with statements.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How does a guarantor get a CIB report?

2. <a id="what-you-can-bring-home-item-2"></a>**Apply at the PKB branch where the business is** <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>; fees are 0.5% processing and 0.5% documentation, each at most Tk 15,000, and the decision comes within 15 working days of a complete application (16238, or +880 9677-787878 from abroad) <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>.
3. <a id="what-you-can-bring-home-item-3"></a>**If the branch doesn't resolve a problem,** complain in this order:
   1. PKB's grievance officer, the General Manager (Administration and Accounts) (gm2@pkb.gov.bd, +88-02-48321195, 30 working days) <sup>[39](#o39)</sup>.
   2. Its Deputy Managing Director (dmd@pkb.gov.bd, +88-02-48319468, 20 working days) <sup>[39](#o39)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Can an applicant refused a PKB reintegration loan appeal the decision?
   > - Can a refused applicant reapply?
